Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a comprehensive analysis of the architecture and constraints for feature planning.

## Architecture Overview

The game has three **hard-separated, independently-testable layers**:

1. **GameEngine** (`lib/game/engine/`, ~4.1K LOC) — headless simulation with no DOM, Three.js, or WebAudio. All game state, commands, physics, mobs, combat, and step logic live here. Runs in `bun test` headlessly.

2. **GameRenderer** (`lib/game/render/`, ~224K) — maps engine state to Three.js GPU objects each frame. Controls camera, world meshing, particles, sky, projectiles, player model (third-person), and held items. Pure state-to-render mapping via `sync(state)`.

3. **AudioDirector** (`lib/game/audio/`, ~112K) — maps engine events and state to synthesized sound via WebAudio + ZZFX. No audio files; all SFX and music generated procedurally at runtime.

The **React shell** (`useMinecraftGame`, `components/`, ~43 TSX files) reads immutable `GameSnapshot`s via `useSyncExternalStore` and dispatches `Command`s. A single `requestAnimationFrame` loop (capped at 50ms substeps) maintains frame coherence.

**World layer** (`lib/world/`, ~148K) handles voxel storage, deterministic terrain generation, lighting (derived cache, never serialized), meshing with AO, and spatial queries (raycasts, collisions).

## Content Extension Points (Very Thorough)

The docs are precise about adding content. You extend via **data tables + one-seam integration**:

### Blocks
- Add to `BlockId` enum + `BLOCK_COLORS` (atlas auto-generates)
- `BREAK_HARDNESS`, `ITEM_DEFS` (blockId), `BLOCK_TO_SLOT` mapping
- Map to `MaterialGroup` in audio materials (exhaustive, enforced by typecheck)
- Assign lighting class (`opacity`, `emission`) in `lighting.ts`
- Non-cube/transparent blocks need collision + meshing work (doors & glass are references)

### Items & Recipes
- `ITEM_DEFS` (kind, stats: tools get `minePower`/`mineTier`/`maxDurability`, weapons get `attack`, armor gets `armorSlot`/`defense`, food gets `hunger`)
- 16×16 inventory sprite via `ITEM_SPRITE_GRIDS` or auto-generation for named prefixes
- `RECIPES` (optional `station` gates it: `"furnace"` smelting, `"villager"` trades, `"brewing"` potions)
- Station support requires 4-place union extension (Recipe.station, GameState.craftingStation, GameSnapshot.craftingStation, openedStation event)

### Mobs
- `MOB_TEMPLATES` (hp, detectRange for passive/hostile, model colors, ranged flag)
- `MOB_DROPS` (itemId min/max/chance)
- `MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS` + `MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS` (ZZFX params)
- `CALL_INTERVALS` (ambient call timer)
- **All five tables are exhaustive Records keyed by MobKind; typecheck enforces lockstep**
- Spawning wired in `spawnDirector.ts` (initial or night trickle); breeding is generic once in `FEED_ITEMS`

### Combat & Projectiles
- Arrows/projectiles: call `spawnArrow(state, x, y, z, dir, {speed, damage, knockback, fromPlayer, ttl})`
- Ranged weapon: gate on held item in `attack` command, spend durability
- Ranged mob: set `ranged: true` on template; `mobAI.ts` makes it kite and fire
- Renderer auto-renders arrows via `projectileVisuals.ts`

### Mechanics & Systems
- New mechanic = new module in `lib/game/engine/systems/` + slot in `GameEngine.step` step order + tunables in `config.ts`
- **Fixed step order** (documented in architecture.md):
  - Pause gate → stuck detection → death/respawn → sleep fade → player motion → hunger/regen/hazards → mining → day-night → weather → random ticks → spawning → mob AI → projectiles → breeding
  - Each system is a function over `GameState`, never mutates in place
  - Tests live adjacent (`.test.ts`)

### Interactive Blocks (Chests, Beds, Furnaces, Doors)
- Register in `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS` + add handler in `tryInteractBlock`
- Return `true` to consume the click (prevent block placement)
- **Block-entity storage** (chests): `state.containers: Map<voxelIndex, InventorySlot[]>`; opening sets `state.openContainerIndex`
- Persistence: non-empty containers serialized as `blockEntities` (additive save bump)

### Worldgen & Structures
- Placed last in `generateWorld` pipeline (`lib/world/generation.ts`)
- Mirror `placeHouse`/`placeDungeons` — loop GEN count, validate, write blocks
- **Any block write changes byte-identical output** → re-baseline hash tests + bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION`
- Dungeon loot: fill lazily on first access, seeded from `world.seed ^ voxelIndex`, gate re-roll on **looted set not emptiness**

### Status Effects
- Add id to `EffectId` union + `EFFECT_ORDER` in `config.ts`
- **Apply at exactly one seam**: Speed multiplies speed, Strength adds to melee, Fire Resist gates lava, Poison/Regen run in `tickStatusEffects`
- Persist via `effects` field (cleared on death)
- UI shows via `effectsProjection` in GameEngine → `GameSnapshot.activeEffects`

### Enchantments
- Add id to `EnchantmentId` union + `ENCHANTMENT_DEFS` + `ENCHANTMENT_ORDER`
- **Read at one seam**: `sharpnessBonus`, `equippedDefense`, `miningSpeed`, etc.
- Persist on `InventorySlot.enchantments` (validated on load)
- Apply via `enchant` command at enchanting table

## System Maturity Analysis

**Mature systems** (20+ test files, extensive tunables, proven stable):
- **Combat** — 7 ranged/melee operators, bow + spears, skeleton kiting, boss with phases, arrows with swept collision + gravity
- **Mob AI** — 8+ mob kinds, aggro/flee/wander, line-of-sight, daylight burning, dungeon spawners
- **Farming & breeding** — random-tick crop growth, sapling→tree, grass spread, animal pairing + baby growth
- **Inventory & recipes** — 36 slots + hotbar, 9-slot chest, durability + enchantments + custom names, 50+ recipes
- **Mining, enchanting, anvil, grindstone** — full tool/armor progression, XP, gear repair/upgrade
- **Fishing** — rod durability, bobber physics, tethering, loot table

**Moderate systems** (well-defined, few edge cases):
- **Lighting** — per-voxel sky + block light, opacity/emission, lazy patching on edits
- **Day-night cycle** — 240 s cycle, daylight formula, hostiles spawn/burn gates, weather cosmetic overlay
- **Physics** — gravity, jump, walk/sprint/crouch, collision, raycasting (DDA)
- **Audio** — ZZFX synth, per-material SFX, music mood + biome with hysteresis

**Thin systems** (basic scaffolding, few hooks):
- **Game modes** — `gameMode` flag, intent predicates (`takesDamage`, `canEditBlocks`, `canFly`), in-game switchable
- **Difficulty** — `difficulty` axis, per-level multipliers for spawning/damage/regen, Peaceful despawns hostiles
- **Hardcore** — boolean flag, immutable, forces Survival + Hard, sets `gameOver` flag on death
- **Third-person camera** — boom camera + occlusion culling; first-person and boom selectable via V key

## Tuning & Balance Categories

`config.ts` has ~70 constants organized into:
- Physics (jump, gravity, speeds)
- Survival pressure (hunger drain, regen gates, drowning, lava)
- Day-night danger (spawn thresholds 0.28–0.72 daylight, mob caps, spawner drip)
- Mob stats (health, damage, arrow speed, boss phases)
- Explosions (power, fuse timers, chain delay)
- Farming pace (random-tick interval, crop growth chance, breeding caps)
- XP & enchanting (cost, magnitude per level)
- Rendering (RENDER_RADIUS 90, RENDER_GRID 20)

No gameplay tuning happens outside `config.ts` — item stats live in `items.ts` (tier progression), worldgen constants in frozen `GEN` object (hash-pinned).

## Critical Technical Constraints

### Save Format Compatibility
- **Saves store diffs against generated terrain** — changing worldgen or the voxel index formula (`x + z*sizeX + y*sizeX*sizeZ`) silently corrupts saves
- Bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (currently 8) when terrain changes; old worlds discard stale diffs and regenerate from seed
- Worldgen output is pinned by SHA-256 tests in `generation.test.ts`; code changes require re-baseline
- Save schema versioning is independent (currently v12, with migrations for old formats)

### Zero Binary Assets Rule
- Block textures, item sprites, UI tiles all painted from TypeScript pixel code
- All sound synthesized at runtime (ZZFX SFX + procedural music via WebAudio nodes)
- **Only exception**: the Monocraft font (OFL-licensed WOFF2, self-hosted via next/font/local)
- Implication: new visuals must be procedurally generated or added to existing sprite/tile tables

### Client-Only, LocalStorage Saves
- No server, no backend, no auth
- All data persists in localStorage (`minecraft_world_save_<worldId>`, `minecraft_profiles_v1`, `minecraft_audio_v1`)
- Multi-world + multi-profile support (no cloud sync, no cross-device play)
- Session-only state (mobs, projectiles, fishing bobber, boss, transient timers) is never serialized

### Engine Invariants (Easy to Break Silently)
1. **Transparent blocks are explicit render layers** — glass goes to a blended, non-depth-writing material; opaque+water stay normal. Lava is an exception (solid, normal face-culling). New transparent blocks need routing changes in `buildGeometryLayersRegion`.

2. **Lighting is a derived cache** — never serialized, always baked/patched locally in the engine (never renderer). Every new block needs `opacity` and `emission` classification.

3. **Doors are multi-cell state machines** — 4 facings × open/closed × upper/lower = 16 block IDs. Placement/breaking atomically updates both cells. Custom bounds drive collision and meshing.

4. **Being in water is not "stuck"** — don't count water as collision; players may stay indefinitely submerged (but take armor-bypassing damage after 60 s continuous exposure). Water-exposure and drowning timers are transient, reset on reload/respawn.

5. **Water is replaceable during placement** — raycasts ignore water and target the solid surface behind it; placement accepts water-containing cells.

6. **DDA raycast boundary ambiguity** — a ray origin exactly on a cell boundary may target a diagonal neighbor (relevant for test assertions).

7. **Pointer lock: first click only acquires lock (no mining)** — KeyI exits explicitly; right-click places without lock.

8. **No DOM/window/localStorage at module top level** — everything is "use client" and mounts via callback ref/effect; the engine stays DOM-free so it runs under `bun test`.

### Performance Budgets
- Renderer meshes a region of `RENDER_RADIUS` (90 blocks) around the player; rebuilds every `RENDER_GRID` (20 blocks) of movement or on `state.worldMeshDirty`
- Random-tick sampler: `RANDOM_TICK_SAMPLES` (64) columns per `RANDOM_TICK_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (0.5), within `RANDOM_TICK_RADIUS` (32)
- Mobs capped globally at `HOSTILE_CAP` (16, scaled by difficulty) + `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP` (6 per dungeon room)
- Projectile anti-tunnel substepping: `ARROW_MAX_SUBSTEPS` (4), `ARROW_MAX_SEGMENT` (0.5 blocks)

## Directory Structure & LOC Breakdown

```
lib/game/
  engine/           4.1K  headless step loop + commands + block diff tracking
    systems/        3.3K  33 system modules (mining, combat, mobs, spawn, etc.)
  render/          224K  Three.js scene, camera, mesh, particles, HUD
  audio/           112K  ZZFX synth, procedural music, footsteps, ambient
  (configs/data)   1.2K  items.ts (406 LOC), recipes.ts (633 LOC), mobs.ts (129 LOC), etc.

lib/world/
  meshing.ts, generation.ts, lighting.ts, queries.ts, blocks.ts

components/           43 TSX files  menu shell, inventory, HUD, pause, crafting panels

Tests:
  21 .test.ts files in lib/game/
  5 .test.ts files in lib/world/
  13 .test.ts in engine/systems/
  E2E smoke suite (Playwright, lib/game/* state assertions)
```

## Feature Planning Guidance

**Feasible additions** (follow existing patterns, no engine changes):
- New blocks, items, recipes (extend data tables)
- New mobs (five exhaustive records, no custom logic needed initially)
- New status effects (one seam per effect, config tunables)
- New enchantments (one seam, per-level modifier)
- Random-tick behaviors (crops, saplings, grass — handler registration)
- Interactive blocks (door/bed/chest references) — including container blocks
- Simple worldgen structures (houses, dungeons model)
- Fishing / loot tables (pure data tables + roll function)

**Moderate lift** (requires system extension):
- New game mechanic (new system module + step slot + tunables + tests)
- Ranged mob or new combat pattern (branching in mob AI)
- Explosion-like behavior (reuse `explode()`, adjust power/fuses)
- New damage type or hazard (add status effect or seam in `playerStats.ts`)

**Significant work** (engine changes, save format, or hash pinning):
- New rendering feature (particle types, post-processing, new material/shader)
- New worldgen terrain feature (re-baseline hashes, bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION`)
- Procedural changes to save schema (add migration, test compatibility)
- Transparent or non-cube block shapes (collision + meshing rewiring)

**Constraints to keep in mind:**
- No binary assets (procedural generation or existing sprite/sound tables only)
- No server/backend (client-only, localStorage saves)
- Worldgen changes require hash re-baselining and version bumps
- Every new `MobKind` touches 5 exhaustive Record tables — typecheck catches missing entries
- Game loop step order is fixed; each system reads state at a precise tick, writes idempotently
- Serialization is conservative: only schema bumps for additive fields, migrations for breaking changes

This is a very well-architected, mature indie game engine optimized for rapid content iteration. The extension patterns are clear, test coverage is solid, and constraints are explicit in the docs.

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a comprehensive inventory of implemented features in the Monecraft voxel game:

## GAMEPLAY FEATURES INVENTORY

### 1. BLOCKS (37 types)
**Registry: `/lib/world/blocks.ts` (BlockId enum)**

Basic blocks: Grass, Dirt, Sand, Snow, Cactus, Wood, Leaves, Planks, Stone, Cobblestone, Brick, Glass
Ores (6 tiers): Coal, Sliver, Ruby, Gold, Sapphire, Diamond
Interaction stations: Furnace, Chest (27 slots), Brewing Stand, Enchanting Table, Anvil, Grindstone
Decorative/Functional: Bed (with spawn point setting), Farmland, Wheat (4 growth stages), Sapling (auto-grows into trees), Torch (light source, crafted), TNT (explosive), Door (wooden, 8 states: 4 facings × open/closed, 2 heights), Moss Cobblestone (dungeon decoration only)
Environmental: Water, Lava (worldgen-only hazard), Bedrock (unbreakable floor), Air
Hardness values range from 1 (farmland, torches) to 30 (spawners)

### 2. ITEMS, TOOLS, WEAPONS, ARMOR
**Registry: `/lib/game/items.ts` (ITEM_DEFS array)**

**Tools (9 pickaxes)**: Wood → Stone → Sliver → Ruby → Sapphire → Gold → Diamond (7-tier progression)
Plus: Fishing Rod (durable, casts to water), Wood Hoe (for tilling farmland)

**Weapons (21 types)**:
- Swords (8 tiers): Knife, Wood, Stone, Sliver, Ruby, Sapphire, Gold, Diamond (attack 9–47, durability 50–720)
- Spears (8 tiers): throwable projectiles with extended melee reach (~7 blocks vs 4.5 for swords), separate throw damage
- Bow: ranged weapon, fires arrows, durability 200
- Dragon Sword: endgame best-in-game melee weapon (attack 60, durability 1200)

**Armor (6 slots, not stackable)**:
- Helmet, Face Mask, Neck Protection, Chestplate, Leggings, Boots
- Defense values: 2–4 per piece
- Made from sapphire/ruby/gold ore combinations

**Food (10 types)**:
- Raw meats (3 hunger): Raw Chicken, Mutton, Beef, Porkchop, Fish
- Cooked meats (8 hunger), Bread (6), Rotten Flesh (2, poisons you 80% of the time)
- Generic Food item (7 hunger)

**Crafting materials**: Wool, Feather, Bone, Bone Meal (fertilizer), Leather, String, Coal, Charcoal, Gunpowder, Emerald (trade currency)

**Potions (5 types, drunk with F key)**:
- Swiftness (3 min, move ×1.2)
- Strength (3 min, +3 melee damage)
- Regeneration (45 sec, heal 1 HP/1.5s even at low hunger)
- Fire Resistance (3 min, immune to lava)
- Water Breathing (3 min, no drowning)

Plus: Arrows, Empty Bottles, Dragon Heart (boss drop)

### 3. MOBS & ENTITIES (10 types + Boss)
**Registry: `/lib/game/mobs.ts` (MOB_TEMPLATES object)**

**Passive animals** (can be bred with food; ~8% drop saplings from leaves):
- Sheep (HP 10, drops wool + mutton)
- Chicken (HP 7, drops feather + chicken)
- Horse (HP 14, drops leather)
- Cow (HP 10, drops leather + beef)
- Pig (HP 8, drops porkchop)
- Villager (HP 20, non-combat NPC, trades emeralds for materials)

**Hostile mobs** (spawn at night, loot tables in `/lib/game/mobLoot.ts`):
- Zombie (HP 100, 3 damage, drops rotten flesh)
- Skeleton (HP 100, ranged/kiting, shoots arrows, drops bone)
- Spider (HP 100, only hostile in dark below daylight 0.42, drops string)
- Creeper (HP 100, explodes with 3-block radius when fused, drops gunpowder)

**Boss** (summoned via Cursed Totem):
- Towering dark figure (HP 1000)
- Melees (10 damage), shoots 3-arrow spread (7 damage each), summons minions (up to 4)
- Drops Dragon Heart + 2–4 diamond ore

Mob AI in `/lib/game/engine/systems/mobAI.ts`: detection ranges, melee/ranged behaviors, kiting, breeding, fleeing

### 4. CRAFTING & SMELTING SYSTEMS
**Registries:**
- Crafting: `/lib/game/recipes.ts` (68 recipes)
- Smelting: furnace recipes (charcoal, cooked meats)
- Brewing: 5 potion recipes at brewing stand
- Trading: `/lib/game/trades.ts` (10 villager trades)

**Key systems**:
- Furnace (station-gated, opens smelting panel)
- Brewing Stand (crafted: 3 cobble + 1 gold ore, opens potion brewing)
- Crafting grid (always available in inventory)
- Recipe book with categories (Tools, Weapons, Armor, Building, Food, Materials, Smelting, Brewing, Trades)
- Recipes show as "craftable now" vs locked with ingredient hints

### 5. SURVIVAL MECHANICS
**Health & Hunger** (`/lib/game/config.ts`):
- Health: 20 HP (10 hearts)
- Hunger: 20 points (10 drumsticks)
- Hunger drain: ~1 per 100 blocks sprinted, ~1 per 300 blocks walked, ~1 per 50 jumps
- Health regen only at hunger ≥12, ~0.5 HP/3 sec
- Sprinting requires hunger >6
- Starvation at hunger 0: 1 HP/4 sec down to difficulty-scaled floor (Peaceful never, Easy at 5 hearts, Normal at 0.5, Hard kills)

**Hazards**:
- Drowning: 15 sec to empty breath bar underwater, then 1 HP/sec (drains fast, refills fast on surfacing)
- Water immersion damage: 60 sec grace period, then 1.5 hearts/sec armor-bypassing
- Lava: 3 hearts/0.5 sec immediate contact, armor-bypassing, continues burning 3 sec after leaving
- Fall damage (standard gravity-based physics)
- Status effects: Poison from rotten flesh (never lethal, floors at 0.5 hearts)

**Combat**:
- Melee reach: 4.5 blocks (spears: 7 blocks)
- Attack aim: must face target within 0.89 dot product
- Bare fist: 6 damage
- Weapon durability consumed per hit
- Knockback on hit
- Mob aggro ranges: Zombie 11, Skeleton 12, Creeper 12, Boss 28 blocks

**Day-night cycle** (4 min per full day, `/lib/game/engine/systems/dayNight.ts`):
- Hostile spawn threshold: daylight <0.28
- Spider aggro threshold: daylight <0.42
- Hostile burn threshold: daylight >0.72
- Sleep allowed at night; resets day clock

**Weather** (cosmetic, not persistent):
- Rain/snow cycles with intensity envelope
- 35% rain chance, 180 sec weather window

### 6. WORLD GENERATION
**Registry: `/lib/world/generation.ts` (GEN constants, WorldType enum)**

**World types**:
- Default: balanced terrain with all biomes
- Superflat: flat ground for builders (caves/ores still below)
- Amplified: 2× exaggerated relief (mountains, valleys)
- Islands: raised sea splitting land into archipelago

**Biomes (5 types)**: Plains, Desert, Ocean, Forest, Mountains
- Each biome has baseHeight + noiseScale for terrain variation
- Mountain peaks spawn stone above Y=65, snow above Y=68
- Sand beaches 1 block above / 2 below sea level
- Sea level: Y=43 (adjustable per world type)

**Features generated**:
- Caves (230 caves, 150 chambers, seeded carving)
- Ores (5 types): Coal (shallow), Sliver, Ruby, Gold, Sapphire, Diamond (deep)
- Lava pools (Y≤9, deep caves only)
- Trees (9000 attempts, biome-dependent spawn chance 0.01–0.6)
- Cacti (desert only, 1.2% per column)
- Houses (120 structures, scattered)
- Dungeons (28 spawner rooms with 1–2 loot chests)
- Sapling growth system: random ticks advance growth, instant with bone meal

**Terrain determinism**:
- Seeded PRNG for reproducible generation
- WORLDGEN_VERSION=8 (bumped when generation changes; old saves re-gen from seed)

### 7. INVENTORY & HOTBAR SYSTEMS
**Registry: `/lib/game/inventory.ts`, `/lib/game/save.ts`**

- Hotbar: 9 slots (always visible, quick-select with 1–9 keys)
- Inventory: 36 slots (opened with I key)
- Chest storage: 27 slots (single-chest equivalent)
- Max stack: 99 items (tools/weapons/armor non-stackable, count 1 per slot)
- Armor slots: 6 dedicated equip slots (helmet, face_mask, neck_protection, chestplate, leggings, boots)
- Open/close chest interaction (`/lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`)
- Chest contents persist until mined/broken

### 8. XP & ENCHANTING SYSTEM
**Registry: `/lib/game/enchantments.ts`, `/lib/game/engine/systems/xp.ts`**

**XP sources** (`/lib/game/mobXp.ts`):
- Mob kills: passive 1–2, hostile 5, boss 200
- Ore mining: coal 1, sliver 2, ruby/gold 4, sapphire 6, diamond 8
- Fishing: 2 per catch

**Enchantments** (5 types, 3 levels max except Mending=1):
- Sharpness: weapons, +2 damage/level
- Protection: armor, +2 defense/level
- Efficiency: tools, ×(1 + 0.3 × level) mining speed
- Unbreaking: all durable gear, 20% skip wear per level
- Mending: all durable gear, 2 durability repair per XP diverted

**Enchanting mechanics**:
- Cost: 3 XP levels per application (1 for Mending)
- Applied at Enchanting Table (crafted: 2 diamond ore + 4 cobble)
- Persists across save/death
- Per-item instance data (stored with slot)

**Grindstone** (`/lib/game/grindstone.ts`):
- Strips all enchantments from an item
- Refunds 5 XP points per enchantment level removed
- Crafted: 2 cobble + 2 planks

**Anvil** (`/lib/game/anvil.ts`):
- Repair with ore/material (25% durability per unit)
- Combine duplicate items (merges enchants, repairs, 12% bonus)
- Rename items (anvil name shown in inventory/death screen)
- Costs: 1 level to repair, 4 to combine, 1 to rename

### 9. ADVANCED SURVIVAL FEATURES

**Fishing** (`/lib/game/engine/systems/fishing.ts`, `/lib/game/fishingLoot.ts`):
- Cast from hotbar at water within 7 blocks reach
- Bite window: 2–5 sec random wait, then 1.2 sec reel window
- Catch: mostly raw fish, sometimes junk (string, bone, seeds), rarely treasure (emerald, leather)
- Rod durability 64, consumed per cast

**Farming** (`/lib/game/engine/systems/randomTicks.ts`):
- Hoe tills dirt/grass to farmland (blocks placed with right-click)
- Plant wheat seeds on farmland
- Wheat grows through 4 stages over ~2.5 min (random ticks)
- Mature wheat drops wheat + 1–2 seeds
- Bone meal: 1 bone → 3 meal; applies to advance crops 1–2 stages or grow saplings instantly

**Animal Breeding** (`/lib/game/engine/systems/breeding.ts`):
- Feed passive animals (wheat to sheep/cows/horses, seeds to chickens/pigs)
- Puts them "in love" for 30 sec within 3 blocks
- Baby born after ~90 sec, grows to adult
- Passive cap: 24

**Dungeon loot** (`/lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts`):
- Common chest (75%): bone (always), bread, cooked chicken, string, seeds, sliver/gold ore, stone pickaxe (25%)
- Rare chest (25%): common roll + diamond/sapphire ore (30–35%), ruby/sapphire sword (15%), helmet/chestplate (20%), diamond pickaxe (8%)
- Chests seeded per location; same loot until opened

**Spawners** (`/lib/game/engine/systems/dungeon.ts`):
- Drip one hostile every ~8 sec while player within 16 blocks
- Up to 6 clustered per spawner (global hostile cap 16)
- Mining spawner block (very hard, 30 hardness) stops it

### 10. AUDIO/MUSIC SYSTEM
**Registry: `/lib/game/audio/`**

Files present:
- `audioDirector.ts`: orchestrates sound playback (reactions to events)
- `musicBrain.ts`: dynamic music system (state-driven)
- `musicPlayer.ts`: music playback control
- `soundParams.ts`: comprehensive sound effect definitions (footsteps, mining, placing, combat, etc.)
- `materials.ts`: block-specific audio properties
- `mobAmbience.ts`: mob sounds
- `rainLoop.ts`: weather audio
- `footsteps.ts`: player footstep system
- Synthesis via `zzfx.d.ts` (ZzFX web audio library)

**All sound & music synthesized at runtime, no audio files.**

### 11. MULTIPLAYER & PERSISTENCE
**Single-player only** (confirmed in code): `useMinecraftGame.ts` notes "single-player client game"

**Save/Load system** (`/lib/game/save.ts`, `/lib/game/worlds.ts`):
- Multi-world support: each profile has own list
- Multi-profile support: each browser user can have own profile + worlds
- localStorage persistence (browser-based)
- Autosave every 15 sec
- Save schema: v11 (tracking player state, inventory, armor, effects, containers, boss state, etc.)
- World state includes: block changes (deltas), player position/inventory, etc.
- Dead worlds: Hardcore mode death makes world spectator-only, persists as "dead world"
- Legacy migration: `legacyMigration.ts` handles v1–v11 save migrations

**Game modes** (`/lib/game/gameModes.ts`, switchable anytime):
- **Survival**: full rules (hunger, health, mobs, block placement/breaking)
- **Creative**: invincible, instant break, no hunger, fly, palette of all items
- **Adventure**: survival rules but no block breaking/placing (explore only)
- **Spectator**: noclip camera, invisible, invulnerable, no interaction

**Difficulty** (switchable anytime):
- **Peaceful**: no hostiles, 2× regen, starvation stops at 10 HP
- **Easy**: fewer hostiles (cap 8), ×0.5 damage, starve stops at 5 hearts
- **Normal**: baseline (cap 16), ×1.0 damage, starve stops at 0.5 hearts
- **Hard**: more hostiles (cap 24), ×1.5 damage, starvation kills

**Hardcore** (immutable once set):
- Locks game mode to Survival + difficulty to Hard
- Permadeath: on death, world becomes spectator-only "dead world"
- Reddened heart display indicating hardcore mode
- Game Over screen (Spectate / Back to Worlds / Delete World)

## FILE PATHS FOR KEY REGISTRIES

- **Blocks**: `/lib/world/blocks.ts` (BlockId enum)
- **Items**: `/lib/game/items.ts` (ITEM_DEFS array, BREAK_HARDNESS, BLOCK_TO_SLOT, REPAIR_MATERIAL_BY_ITEM)
- **Mobs**: `/lib/game/mobs.ts` (MOB_TEMPLATES, mobHalfHeight)
- **Mob loot**: `/lib/game/mobLoot.ts` (MOB_DROPS)
- **Dungeon loot**: `/lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts` (DUNGEON_LOOT tiers)
- **Fishing loot**: `/lib/game/fishingLoot.ts` (FISHING_LOOT)
- **Crafting recipes**: `/lib/game/recipes.ts` (CRAFTING_RECIPES, groupRecipes)
- **Villager trades**: `/lib/game/trades.ts` (TRADES)
- **Enchantments**: `/lib/game/enchantments.ts` (ENCHANTMENT_DEFS, seam readers)
- **World generation**: `/lib/world/generation.ts` (GEN constants, WorldType enum)
- **Main config (tuning)**: `/lib/game/config.ts` (all gameplay constants)
- **Game modes**: `/lib/game/gameModes.ts` (GameMode union, predicates)
- **Difficulties**: `/lib/game/difficulties.ts` (Difficulty union, accessors)
- **Save/load**: `/lib/game/save.ts` (migration chain, schema), `/lib/game/worlds.ts` (multi-world persistence)

## WHAT'S NOT IMPLEMENTED

- No achievements/statistics tracking
- No quests or objectives (except implicit win: defeat boss)
- No multiplayer/networking
- No modding API
- No respawning mobs after kill (mobs drop loot once, despawn)
- No mob equipment/drops of held items
- No potion effects beyond 5 types (no Poison as drinkable potion)
- No redstone/circuits
- No player-made contraptions (no hoppers, dispensers, comparators, etc.)
- No biome-exclusive blocks/ores (same ore distribution everywhere)
- No custom dimension/portals (Nether/End-style)
- No villages/raids
- No experience orbs (XP is invisible, banks as points)

## Where Monecraft is today (v0.10.0 + unreleased armor work, save v12)

It's a remarkably complete survival voxel game already: 7-tier tool/weapon/armor progression, 6 mobs + a summonable boss, crafting/smelting/brewing/villager trading, caves with per-voxel lighting, 5 biomes, dungeons, farming, breeding, fishing, **XP + enchanting + anvil/grindstone/mending** (just shipped), game modes, difficulty, and hardcore. The architecture is clean and content-extension is well-paved (data tables + one-seam integration).

## What's missing / natural next steps

Given the momentum from the recent XP/enchanting/armor work and the codebase's constraints (zero binary assets, client-only, procedural), the strongest candidates cluster into four directions:

1. **Deepen combat & progression** *(low risk, builds on what just shipped)* — The enchanting system only has 5 enchantments and there are only 5 potion effects. Adding Fortune, Looting, Power, Fire Aspect, Knockback, Feather Falling, plus Night Vision / Jump Boost / Invisibility / splash & harming potions is nearly all "one-seam" work with no worldgen impact. High payoff per unit effort.

2. **A new dimension (Nether-style)** *(highest excitement, biggest lift)* — Portal blocks linking to a separate hellish layer with its own worldgen, blocks, ores, hostile mobs, and materials. Real endgame depth, but touches worldgen + save format + rendering.

3. **Living world** *(explicitly roadmapped)* — Generated **villages** with villager professions + occasional **raids**, plus **tameable wolf/cat companions** that fight alongside you (extends the existing breeding + mob-AI systems). The CHANGELOG already flags villages as planned future work.

4. **Goals & variety** *(medium effort, broad appeal)* — An **Advancements/achievements** panel + **statistics** screen to give players direction, plus new biomes (jungle/swamp/savanna) and crops (carrots, potatoes, pumpkins, melons).

My personal lean: **#1 first** as a quick high-value win that capitalizes on the enchanting/potion infrastructure you just built, then **#3 (Living world)** as the next meatier feature since villages are already roadmapped.

# Implementation Plan: "Deepen Progression" — More Enchantments & Effects

## What I confirmed about the seams (verified by reading)

**Enchantments** (`lib/game/enchantments.ts`): each enchant is a flat per-level modifier read at exactly one seam. `ENCHANTMENT_DEFS` is keyed by `EnchantmentId`, gated by `kinds: ItemKind[]` and `maxLevel`. The `enchant` command (`GameEngine.ts:441`) and `EnchantingColumn.tsx` are fully generic over `EnchantmentId` — adding a union member surfaces it in the UI and command automatically. Seam readers (`sharpnessBonus`, `protectionDefense`, `efficiencyMultiplier`, `unbreakingSkips`, `mendXp`) are the only places magnitudes are read.

**Effects** (`EffectId` in `types.ts:12`, `EFFECT_ORDER` in `statusEffects.ts:24`): each effect is read at one seam — Speed multiplies `playerMotion.ts:92`, Strength adds at the melee dispatch `GameEngine.ts:536`, Fire Resistance/Water Breathing are booleans into `tickLavaExposure`/`tickOxygen`, Regen/Poison run inside `tickStatusEffects`. `drinkPotion` (`GameEngine.ts:430`) is generic over `slot.effect.id`. HUD: `EFFECT_ICONS`/`EFFECT_LABELS` in `ActiveEffects.tsx` + a procedural grid case in `hudPixels.ts`.

**The exact damage/yield/physics seams I traced:**

| Mechanic | Seam (file:line) | Has access to |
|---|---|---|
| Melee damage | `GameEngine.ts:536` `weaponDamage+strengthBonus+sharpnessBonus` | held weapon, state |
| Melee knockback | `combat.ts:76-82` `tryAttackMob` (fixed 0.75 impulse) | weapon passed in via dispatch |
| Bow damage/knockback | `combat.ts:108-114` `tryFireBow` → `spawnArrow({damage, knockback})` | held bow, state |
| Mob loot | `GameEngine.ts:762` `rollMobDrops(mob.kind, rng)` in `removeMobAt` | held weapon, state |
| Ore drops | `mining.ts:47` `addBlockDrop` → `rollBlockDrops` (items.ts:389) | `tool`, block |
| Mining speed | `mining.ts:124` `dt * miningSpeed(tool) * MINING_RATE` | `tool`, state |
| Defense | `playerLife.ts:17-18` `armorReduction` in `applyDamageWithArmor` | state |
| Fall damage | `playerMotion.ts:144-146` (`applyDamage` on `vyBeforeMove < -14`) | state (incl. `equippedArmor.boots`) |
| Jump velocity | `playerMotion.ts:108` `velocity.y = JUMP_VELOCITY` | state |
| Durability wear | `inventory.ts:89,111` `unbreakingSkips` | slot |
| Drowning | `playerStats.ts:187` `tickOxygen(waterBreathing)` | state |

**Two findings that shape scope:**
1. The bow is `kind: "weapon"` (`items.ts:245`, `attack: 0`). So Power/Punch gated by `kinds:["weapon"]` would wrongly offer on swords. **Fix:** add an optional `itemIds?: string[]` allow-list to `EnchantmentDef`, checked in `canEnchant` (e.g. Power/Punch → `itemIds:["bow"]`). Small additive registry extension.
2. There is **no mob-side status system** — mobs have only `hp`/`direction`. So **Fire Aspect / Flame** (burn-on-hit) need a brand-new mob-burning mechanic. **Thorns** needs the attacker threaded into the damage path (`applyDamage` is a bare `(amount)=>void` with no source). **Night Vision / Invisibility** need the renderer (render state carries `daylight` but not effects; `GameRenderer.ts:605`). These are all deferred.

**Save-format finding (important):** New enchant ids ride the existing `SavedSlot.enchantments`; new effect ids ride the existing top-level `effects`. Both fields already exist and are validated against compile-enforced `Record<…,true>` totality maps (`VALID_ENCHANT_IDS` save.ts:243, `VALID_EFFECT_IDS` save.ts:316). `restoreEnchantments` already clamps to each enchant's own `maxLevel`. **No new fields, no new BlockIds, no worldgen → no save-version bump and no `WORLDGEN_VERSION` change.** This exactly mirrors the shipped Mending precedent (CHANGELOG: "the `mending` id rides the existing per-slot `enchantments`, so no save-format bump is needed"). New ids are backward-compatible (old saves load) and forward-degrading (an older build silently drops unknown ids on load).

---

## Recommended scope

Two PRs, one commit each, both pure one-seam-per-modifier additions with zero worldgen impact and zero save-format bump.

### Cheap one-seam wins (RECOMMENDED — ship these)

**PR 1 — More enchantments (6):**

| Enchant | Seam | New reader | Applies to |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Power** | `combat.ts` `tryFireBow` damage | `powerBonus(bow)` | bow only (`itemIds:["bow"]`) |
| **Punch** | `combat.ts` `tryFireBow` knockback | `punchKnockback(bow)` | bow only |
| **Knockback** | `combat.ts` `tryAttackMob` impulse | `knockbackBonus(weapon)` | weapons (no-op on bow) |
| **Looting** | `mobLoot.ts` `rollMobDrops` count | `lootingBonus(weapon)` | weapons |
| **Fortune** | `items.ts` `rollBlockDrops` (ore drops only) | `fortuneBonus(tool)` | tools |
| **Feather Falling** | `playerMotion.ts` fall-damage formula | `featherFallingReduction(boots)` | armor (boots) |

All six flow through the existing `enchant` command + `EnchantingColumn` UI automatically. Coherent theme: rounds out ranged (Power/Punch), melee utility (Knockback), yield (Looting/Fortune), and survival (Feather Falling) on top of the existing Sharpness/Protection/Efficiency/Unbreaking/Mending.

**PR 2 — More potions/effects (3 positive, engine-only, no renderer):**

| Effect | Seam | New reader |
|---|---|---|
| **Haste** | `mining.ts:124` progress multiplier | `hasteMultiplier(state)` |
| **Resistance** | `playerLife.ts` `applyDamageWithArmor` (armor-mitigated combat damage only) | `resistanceMultiplier(state)` |
| **Jump Boost** | `playerMotion.ts:108` jump velocity | `jumpBoostBonus(state)` |

Each is a drinkable brewed potion (`station:"brewing"` recipe with an existing reagent — no new worldgen). All three are invisible mechanically, so **no renderer change**, only HUD icons.

### Needs a new mechanic (DEFER — call out, don't build here)

- **Fire Aspect / Flame** — require a mob-burning status (mobs have no effect system). New per-mob burn timer + tick + a melee/arrow-hit application seam. Separate slice.
- **Thorns** — requires threading the attacker handle into every damage source (`applyDamage` has no source today). Separate slice.
- **Night Vision** — renderer brightness boost; effects must be plumbed into the render snapshot/`GameRenderer`. Renderer-touching.
- **Invisibility** — renderer (hide third-person model) **and** a mob-detection seam in `mobAI.ts`. Two-system lift.
- **Splash / Lingering potions** — thrown-projectile-with-area-effect; a whole new projectile+AoE mechanic. This is the gate for the **negative** effects (Slowness, Weakness, Mining Fatigue, drinkable Poison, Harming): without a delivery vector they're self-harm only, so they belong with the splash slice.
- **Healing/Harming (instant)** — small but needs a new `ItemEffect` "instant" shape + a branch in `drinkPotion` (heal/harm now vs. timed). Cheap fast-follow; not in the core slice to keep it purely additive-enum.
- **Respiration** — would scale `OXYGEN_HOLD_SECONDS` in `tickOxygen`; doable engine-only but lower value, optional add-on to PR1.
- **Aqua Affinity** — no underwater mining penalty exists to negate; skip (no seam).

---

## Phased breakdown

### PR 1 — "More enchantments"

**Phase 1a — registry + readers** (`enchantments.ts`, `types.ts`, `config.ts`)
- `types.ts`: extend `EnchantmentId` union with `"power" | "punch" | "knockback" | "looting" | "fortune" | "feather_falling"`. Add optional `itemIds?: string[]` to `EnchantmentDef` (in `enchantments.ts`).
- `enchantments.ts`: add the six `ENCHANTMENT_DEFS` entries + `ENCHANTMENT_ORDER` entries; extend `canEnchant` to also honor `def.itemIds` (when present, slot.id must be in it). Add six seam readers mirroring `sharpnessBonus`.
- `config.ts`: new tunables (below).

**Phase 1b — wire each seam** (one call-site each)
- `combat.ts` `tryFireBow`: `damage: BOW_ARROW_DAMAGE + powerBonus(slot)`, `knockback: BOW_KNOCKBACK + punchKnockback(slot)`.
- `combat.ts` `tryAttackMob`: add a `knockback` param; scale the `0.75` impulse by `0.75 + knockbackBonus`. Pass `knockbackBonus(heldWeapon)` from `GameEngine.ts:536` dispatch.
- `mobLoot.ts` `rollMobDrops`: add optional `lootingLevel = 0`; add `Math.floor(rng()*(level+1))` to each entry's count. Pass level from `removeMobAt` reading the held weapon (`lootingBonus`).
- `items.ts` `rollBlockDrops`: add optional `fortuneLevel = 0`; multiply **ore** drops only (gate via a small `FORTUNE_ORE_BLOCKS` set: CoalOre/SliverOre/RubyOre/GoldOre/SapphireOre/DiamondOre). Pass level from `mining.ts addBlockDrop` reading `tool` (`fortuneBonus`).
- `playerMotion.ts` fall seam: multiply the computed fall damage by `(1 - featherFallingReduction(state.equippedArmor.boots))`, clamped. (playerMotion already has `state`.)

**Phase 1c — save validator + docs + tests** (below).

### PR 2 — "More potions & effects"

**Phase 2a — effect plumbing** (`types.ts`, `statusEffects.ts`, `config.ts`)
- `types.ts`: extend `EffectId` with `"haste" | "resistance" | "jump_boost"`.
- `statusEffects.ts`: add to `EFFECT_ORDER`; add three readers (`hasteMultiplier`, `resistanceMultiplier`, `jumpBoostBonus`). No new tick needed (all gates/multipliers).
- `config.ts`: durations + magnitudes.

**Phase 2b — wire seams**
- `mining.ts:124`: `* hasteMultiplier(state)`.
- `playerLife.ts` `applyDamageWithArmor`: scale `mitigated` by `resistanceMultiplier(state)` (combat damage only; leave poison/void/starvation/lava untouched — document this scoping).
- `playerMotion.ts:108`: `velocity.y = JUMP_VELOCITY + jumpBoostBonus(state)`.

**Phase 2c — items, recipes, HUD**
- `items.ts`: three potion `ItemDef`s (`material`, `effect:{id,durationSeconds}`).
- `recipes.ts`: three `station:"brewing"` recipes (proposed reagents, all bikeable: Haste = Glass Bottle + Gold Ore; Resistance = Glass Bottle + Leather; Jump Boost = Glass Bottle + Sliver Ore).
- `hudPixels.ts`: three `HudIconName` union members + grids + `renderHudIconPixels` cases.
- `ActiveEffects.tsx`: add to `EFFECT_ICONS` + `EFFECT_LABELS`.

**Phase 2d — save validator + docs + tests.**

---

## New config tunables (`lib/game/config.ts`)

Place in the existing "XP & enchanting" and "Status effects" blocks:

```
// Enchantments (PR1)
POWER_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL = 2          // +bow arrow damage per level (matches Sharpness)
PUNCH_KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.25    // +bow knockback per level (base BOW_KNOCKBACK 0.6)
KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.4           // +melee knockback impulse per level (base 0.75)
LOOTING_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1         // max extra count rolled per mob-drop entry per level
FORTUNE_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1         // max extra ore items per drop per level
FEATHER_FALLING_REDUCE_PER_LEVEL = 0.15   // fall-damage reduction per level (cap ~0.8)
// (reuse ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL = 3 as each new enchant's maxLevel for registry uniformity)

// Effects (PR2)
EFFECT_HASTE_DURATION = 180;  EFFECT_HASTE_MULTIPLIER = 1.4   // mining speed ×
EFFECT_RESISTANCE_DURATION = 120; EFFECT_RESISTANCE_MULTIPLIER = 0.8  // incoming combat dmg ×
EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_DURATION = 180; EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_VELOCITY = 2.0    // +JUMP_VELOCITY
```

Magnitudes chosen so Jump Boost (8.2→10.2) stays under the `-14` fall-damage threshold on flat ground.

---

## Save-format / migration analysis

**No version bump for either PR.** Both fields (`SavedSlot.enchantments`, top-level `effects`) already exist and are additive + validated. Required save.ts edits are compile-enforced, not schema changes:
- `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS` (save.ts:243): add the six new keys (TS errors until added — the intended safety net). `restoreEnchantments` auto-clamps each to `ENCHANTMENT_DEFS[id].maxLevel`.
- `VALID_EFFECT_IDS` (save.ts:316): add the three new keys.

`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays **8**; `SAVE_KEY` unchanged. New potion items save by string id (additive, like every other item). Document in save-format.md that v12 is unchanged and the new ids are additive enum values on already-validated fields (exactly the Mending pattern). Optional: a `readSave` round-trip test proving an old build drops an unknown new id (forward-degradation) is worth adding even without a bump.

---

- **`docs/tuning.md`** — the new config constants with their effect.
- **`docs/save-format.md`** — a note under v12 that new enchant/effect ids are additive enum values on existing validated fields; no bump; WORLDGEN_VERSION stays 8.
- **`docs/manual.md`** — player-facing: the new enchantments and how to get them, the new potions and their brew recipes/effects.
- **`docs/adding-content.md`** — the "An enchantment" / "A status effect" sections already document the exact pattern; verify the `itemIds` allow-list note is added under the enchantment section (the one genuinely new registry capability).
- **`CHANGELOG.md`** — one `### Added` bullet per PR under `## [Unreleased]`, explicitly stating zero new assets, no worldgen change, and **no save-format bump** (new ids ride existing validated fields).

## Tests to add

**PR 1** — extend `lib/game/enchantments.test.ts` (`describe("seam readers")`):
- `powerBonus`/`punchKnockback`/`knockbackBonus`/`lootingBonus`/`fortuneBonus`/`featherFallingReduction` return flat per-level values; 0/absent on wrong gear.
- `canEnchant` gating: Power/Punch offered on bow, **refused on swords** (new `itemIds` path); Knockback/Looting on weapons; Fortune on tools only.
- `lib/game/mobLoot.test.ts`: `rollMobDrops` with a deterministic rng + Looting raises counts predictably (0→min, level adds bonus).
- `lib/game/items.test.ts` or `mining.test.ts`: `rollBlockDrops` Fortune multiplies an **ore** block but **not** a non-ore (grass/leaves/wheat).
- `lib/game/engine/systems/combat.test.ts`: bow arrow damage includes Power; melee knockback impulse scales with Knockback.
- `playerStats.test.ts` (or a `GameEngine.test.ts` case): Feather Falling reduces fall damage; no boots → unchanged.
- `lib/game/save.test.ts`: round-trip persisting a new enchant id; unknown id dropped; level clamped to maxLevel.

**PR 2** — extend `lib/game/engine/systems/statusEffects.test.ts`:
- `hasteMultiplier`/`resistanceMultiplier`/`jumpBoostBonus` reflect active effects (1/1/0 when inactive).
- `mining.test.ts`: Haste raises mining progress.
- `playerStats.test.ts`/`playerLife`: Resistance reduces armor-mitigated combat damage but **not** poison/void (assert the scoping).
- `playerMotion`/`GameEngine.test.ts`: Jump Boost raises jump velocity.
- `lib/game/recipes.test.ts` already validates that recipe slot ids exist — the three new brewing recipes get covered; add an assertion that each new potion's `effect.id` is a valid `EffectId`.
- `lib/game/save.test.ts`: round-trip a new effect id; unknown effect id dropped.

---

## Sequencing & risks

- PR 1 and PR 2 are independent; ship in either order. Within each, do registry+readers first (compile-driven), then the one-line seam wirings, then UI/docs/tests.
- Lowest-risk items: Power, Punch, Knockback, Jump Boost, Haste, Resistance (single multiply/add at a seam that already has `state`/the slot).
- Two items touch a function signature: **Knockback** adds a param to `tryAttackMob`; **Looting**/**Fortune** add an optional param to `rollMobDrops`/`rollBlockDrops` (default 0 keeps all existing callers and tests green). **Feather Falling** edits the fall-damage formula inside `playerMotion` (a core file) — keep it to a single multiply and cover with a test.
- The only genuinely new registry capability is `EnchantmentDef.itemIds` (to bind Power/Punch to the bow). Keep it optional so all existing defs are untouched.

1	import {
2	  EFFICIENCY_SPEED_PER_LEVEL,
3	  ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL,
4	  MENDING_MAX_LEVEL,
5	  MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP,
6	  PROTECTION_DEFENSE_PER_LEVEL,
7	  SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL,
8	  UNBREAKING_SKIP_PER_LEVEL
9	} from "@/lib/game/config";
10	import { ARMOR_SLOTS } from "@/lib/game/items";
11	import type { Enchantment, EnchantmentId, EquippedArmor, InventorySlot, ItemKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
12	
13	/**
14	 * Enchantments are per-item-instance data (like durability) applied at the
15	 * enchanting table. Each is a flat per-level modifier read at exactly one
16	 * existing seam — the same one-reader-per-seam discipline as status effects:
17	 * Sharpness → melee damage, Protection → equipped defense, Efficiency → mining
18	 * speed, Unbreaking → durability wear. Applies only to durable gear.
19	 */
20	
21	export type EnchantmentDef = {
22	  id: EnchantmentId;
23	  label: string;
24	  /** Item kinds this enchantment can be applied to. */
25	  kinds: ItemKind[];
26	  maxLevel: number;
27	};
28	
29	export const ENCHANTMENT_DEFS: Record<EnchantmentId, EnchantmentDef> = {
30	  sharpness: { id: "sharpness", label: "Sharpness", kinds: ["weapon"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },
31	  protection: { id: "protection", label: "Protection", kinds: ["armor"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },
32	  efficiency: { id: "efficiency", label: "Efficiency", kinds: ["tool"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },
33	  unbreaking: { id: "unbreaking", label: "Unbreaking", kinds: ["tool", "weapon", "armor"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },
34	  mending: { id: "mending", label: "Mending", kinds: ["tool", "weapon", "armor"], maxLevel: MENDING_MAX_LEVEL }
35	};
36	
37	/** Stable display order for the enchanting panel. */
38	export const ENCHANTMENT_ORDER: readonly EnchantmentId[] = ["sharpness", "protection", "efficiency", "unbreaking", "mending"];
39	
40	/** Level of `id` on a slot (0 if absent). */
41	export function enchantLevel(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined, id: EnchantmentId): number {
42	  return slot?.enchantments?.find((e) => e.id === id)?.level ?? 0;
43	}
44	
45	/** Whether `id` can be applied to `slot` now — right item kind, owned, below its max level. */
46	export function canEnchant(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined, id: EnchantmentId): boolean {
47	  if (!slot?.id || !slot.kind || slot.count <= 0) return false;
48	  const def = ENCHANTMENT_DEFS[id];
49	  return def.kinds.includes(slot.kind) && enchantLevel(slot, id) < def.maxLevel;
50	}
51	
52	/** A new slot with `id` added (level 1) or its level bumped by one; returns the slot unchanged if not allowed. */
53	export function applyEnchant(slot: InventorySlot, id: EnchantmentId): InventorySlot {
54	  if (!canEnchant(slot, id)) return slot;
55	  const current = slot.enchantments ?? [];
56	  const enchantments: Enchantment[] = current.some((e) => e.id === id)
57	    ? current.map((e) => (e.id === id ? { id, level: e.level + 1 } : e))
58	    : [...current, { id, level: 1 }];
59	  return { ...slot, enchantments };
60	}
61	
62	// --- Seam readers ---
63	
64	/** Extra melee damage from Sharpness on the held weapon. */
65	export function sharpnessBonus(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined): number {
66	  return enchantLevel(slot, "sharpness") * SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL;
67	}
68	
69	/** Extra defense points from Protection on a worn armor piece. */
70	export function protectionDefense(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined): number {
71	  return enchantLevel(slot, "protection") * PROTECTION_DEFENSE_PER_LEVEL;
72	}
73	
74	/** Mining-speed multiplier from Efficiency on the held tool (1 when absent). */
75	export function efficiencyMultiplier(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined): number {
76	  return 1 + enchantLevel(slot, "efficiency") * EFFICIENCY_SPEED_PER_LEVEL;
77	}
78	
79	/** Whether Unbreaking should skip a point of durability wear this time. */
80	export function unbreakingSkips(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined, rng: () => number): boolean {
81	  const level = enchantLevel(slot, "unbreaking");
82	  return level > 0 && rng() < level * UNBREAKING_SKIP_PER_LEVEL;
83	}
84	
85	/** Repairs one Mending slot with up to `xp` points; returns the repaired slot + XP used, or null when there's nothing to do. */
86	function repairWithXp(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined, xp: number): { slot: InventorySlot; xpUsed: number } | null {
87	  if (!slot?.maxDurability || enchantLevel(slot, "mending") <= 0) return null;
88	  const missing = slot.maxDurability - (slot.durability ?? slot.maxDurability);
89	  if (missing <= 0) return null;
90	  const xpNeeded = Math.ceil(missing / MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP);
91	  const xpUsed = Math.min(xp, xpNeeded);
92	  const repaired = Math.min(missing, xpUsed * MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP);
93	  return { slot: { ...slot, durability: (slot.durability ?? slot.maxDurability) + repaired }, xpUsed };
94	}
95	
96	/**
97	 * Mending seam: diverts gained XP to repair the player's damaged, Mending-enchanted
98	 * gear — the held item first, then worn armor in ARMOR_SLOTS order — at
99	 * MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP durability per diverted XP point. Returns (possibly new)
100	 * slots + equipped records (same refs when nothing was repaired) plus the XP left
101	 * to bank. The one reader for Mending: `awardXp` is its only caller.
102	 */
103	export function mendXp(
104	  slots: InventorySlot[],
105	  selectedSlot: number,
106	  equipped: EquippedArmor,
107	  xp: number
108	): { slots: InventorySlot[]; equipped: EquippedArmor; xpLeft: number } {
109	  if (xp <= 0) return { slots, equipped, xpLeft: xp };
110	  let remaining = xp;
111	  let nextSlots = slots;
112	  let nextEquipped = equipped;
113	
114	  // Held item first (worn armor lives in `equipped`, a disjoint store).
115	  if (remaining > 0 && selectedSlot >= 0 && selectedSlot < slots.length) {
116	    const r = repairWithXp(slots[selectedSlot], remaining);
117	    if (r) {
118	      nextSlots = slots.slice();
119	      nextSlots[selectedSlot] = r.slot;
120	      remaining -= r.xpUsed;
121	    }
122	  }
123	
124	  // Then each worn armor piece.
125	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
126	    if (remaining <= 0) break;
127	    const r = repairWithXp(nextEquipped[armorSlot], remaining);
128	    if (!r) continue;
129	    if (nextEquipped === equipped) nextEquipped = { ...equipped }; // clone on first write
130	    nextEquipped[armorSlot] = r.slot;
131	    remaining -= r.xpUsed;
132	  }
133	
134	  return { slots: nextSlots, equipped: nextEquipped, xpLeft: remaining };
135	}
136	
```

1	import {
2	  EFFECT_REGEN_HP,
3	  EFFECT_REGEN_INTERVAL,
4	  EFFECT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER,
5	  EFFECT_STRENGTH_BONUS,

7	  POISON_HP,
8	  POISON_INTERVAL
9	} from "@/lib/game/config";
10	import type { EffectId } from "@/lib/game/types";
11	import type { EmitGameEvent, GameState } from "../state";
12	
13	/**
14	 * Status-effect core. Effects live in `state.effects` (a Map of id → remaining
15	 * seconds) — session-state that persists across reload (save v6) but is cleared
16	 * on death. Each effect is read by exactly one seam: Speed multiplies movement
17	 * (`playerMotion`), Strength adds melee damage (`GameEngine` attack dispatch),
18	 * Fire Resistance / Water Breathing gate the lava / drowning ticks
19	 * (`playerStats`), and Regeneration / Poison are applied here on their own
20	 * accumulators each tick.
21	 */
22	
23	/** Stable HUD/iteration order for the effects readout. */
24	export const EFFECT_ORDER: readonly EffectId[] = ["speed", "strength", "regeneration", "fire_resistance", "water_breathing", "poison"];
25	
26	export function hasEffect(state: GameState, id: EffectId): boolean {
27	  return (state.effects.get(id) ?? 0) > 0;
28	}
29	
30	export function effectRemaining(state: GameState, id: EffectId): number {
31	  return state.effects.get(id) ?? 0;
32	}
33	
34	/** Adds (or refreshes) an effect to at least `seconds` remaining — never shortens a longer one. */
35	export function addEffect(state: GameState, id: EffectId, seconds: number): void {
36	  if (seconds <= 0) return;
37	  state.effects.set(id, Math.max(state.effects.get(id) ?? 0, seconds));
38	}
39	
40	/** Drops every active effect and resets the periodic accumulators — used on death/respawn. */
41	export function clearEffects(state: GameState): void {
42	  state.effects.clear();
43	  state.timers.effectRegenTimer = 0;
44	  state.timers.effectPoisonTimer = 0;
45	}
46	
47	/** Movement-speed multiplier from the Speed effect (1 when inactive). */
48	export function speedMultiplier(state: GameState): number {
49	  return hasEffect(state, "speed") ? EFFECT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER : 1;
50	}
51	
52	/** Extra melee damage per hit from the Strength effect (0 when inactive). */
53	export function strengthBonus(state: GameState): number {
54	  return hasEffect(state, "strength") ? EFFECT_STRENGTH_BONUS : 0;
55	}
56	
57	export type StatusEffectDeps = {
58	  /** Armor-bypassing, never-lethal damage (poison) — emits playerHurt. */
59	  applyPoisonDamage: (amount: number) => void;
60	  emit: EmitGameEvent;
61	};
62	
63	/**
64	 * Advances every active effect by dt: counts each down (emitting `effectExpired`
65	 * at zero), then applies Regeneration (heals on its own cadence, even at low
66	 * hunger) and Poison (chips away but can't kill). Runs after `tickHealthRegen`
67	 * and before the environmental ticks so the fire-resist / water-breathing gates
68	 * are current when lava / oxygen read them.
69	 */
70	export function tickStatusEffects(state: GameState, dt: number, deps: StatusEffectDeps): void {
71	  for (const [id, remaining] of state.effects) {
72	    const next = remaining - dt;
73	    if (next <= 0) {
74	      state.effects.delete(id);
75	      deps.emit({ type: "effectExpired", effect: id });
76	    } else {
77	      state.effects.set(id, next);
78	    }
79	  }
80	
81	  // Regeneration: heal on an independent accumulator, ignoring the hunger gate.
82	  if (hasEffect(state, "regeneration")) {
83	    state.timers.effectRegenTimer += dt;
84	    while (state.timers.effectRegenTimer >= EFFECT_REGEN_INTERVAL) {
85	      state.timers.effectRegenTimer -= EFFECT_REGEN_INTERVAL;
86	      if (state.hearts < MAX_HEARTS) state.hearts = Math.min(MAX_HEARTS, state.hearts + EFFECT_REGEN_HP);
87	    }
88	  } else {
89	    state.timers.effectRegenTimer = 0;
90	  }
91	
92	  // Poison: never-lethal damage over time (the deps wrapper floors at half a heart).
93	  if (hasEffect(state, "poison")) {
94	    state.timers.effectPoisonTimer += dt;
95	    while (state.timers.effectPoisonTimer >= POISON_INTERVAL) {
96	      state.timers.effectPoisonTimer -= POISON_INTERVAL;
97	      deps.applyPoisonDamage(POISON_HP);
98	    }
99	  } else {
100	    state.timers.effectPoisonTimer = 0;
101	  }
102	}
103	
```

237	    out[armorSlot] = { id: piece.id, count: piece.count, durability: piece.durability, enchantments: piece.enchantments, customName: piece.customName };
238	  }
239	  return out;
240	}
241	
242	// Every known enchantment id, keyed so a new enchantment can't be forgotten here.
243	const VALID_ENCHANT_IDS: Record<EnchantmentId, true> = {
244	  sharpness: true,
245	  protection: true,
246	  efficiency: true,
247	  unbreaking: true,
248	  mending: true
249	};
250	
251	/** Validates persisted enchantments: known ids and integer levels clamped to 1..(that enchant's max); undefined when none survive. */
252	function restoreEnchantments(saved: SavedSlot["enchantments"]): Enchantment[] | undefined {
253	  if (!Array.isArray(saved)) return undefined;
254	  const out: Enchantment[] = [];
255	  for (const entry of saved) {
256	    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== "string" || !Object.hasOwn(VALID_ENCHANT_IDS, entry.id)) continue;
257	    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.level) || Math.floor(entry.level) < 1) continue;
258	    // Clamp to the enchantment's own cap so e.g. a tampered mending:3 loads as 1.
259	    out.push({ id: entry.id, level: Math.min(ENCHANTMENT_DEFS[entry.id].maxLevel, Math.floor(entry.level)) });
260	  }
261	  return out.length > 0 ? out : undefined;
262	}
263	
264	/**
265	 * Rebuilds one slot from its saved snapshot: drops unknown ids, clamps the
266	 * count, and clamps/defaults durability for gear (a non-positive saved
267	 * durability means the piece broke and is dropped). Returns an empty slot for
268	 * anything invalid. Shared by inventory and container restore.
269	 */
270	function restoreSlot(saved: SavedSlot | undefined): InventorySlot {
271	  if (!saved?.id || saved.count <= 0 || !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id]) return createEmptySlot();
272	  const slot = createSlot(saved.id, Math.min(maxStackSizeForItem(saved.id), Math.max(0, Math.floor(saved.count))));
273	  if ((slot.kind === "tool" || slot.kind === "weapon" || slot.kind === "armor") && slot.maxDurability) {
274	    if (typeof saved.durability === "number") {
275	      const loaded = Math.floor(saved.durability);
276	      if (loaded <= 0) return createEmptySlot();
277	      slot.durability = Math.max(1, Math.min(slot.maxDurability, loaded));
278	    } else {
279	      slot.durability = slot.maxDurability;
280	    }
281	    // Enchantments live only on durable gear; validated against the known set.
282	    const enchantments = restoreEnchantments(saved.enchantments);
283	    if (enchantments) slot.enchantments = enchantments;
284	    // A custom name (anvil rename) also lives only on durable gear; trim and cap it.
285	    if (typeof saved.customName === "string") {
286	      const name = saved.customName.trim().slice(0, CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN);
287	      if (name) slot.customName = name;
288	    }
289	  }
290	  return slot;
291	}
292	
293	/** Snapshots non-empty chest containers for persistence (empty chests carry no data). */
294	export function serializeContainers(containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>): SavedContainer[] {
295	  const out: SavedContainer[] = [];
296	  for (const [index, slots] of containers) {
297	    if (!slots.some((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0)) continue;
298	    out.push({ index, slots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(slots) });
299	  }
300	  return out;
301	}
302	
303	/** Snapshots the set of opened/broken dungeon chest voxel indices for persistence. */
304	export function serializeLootedChests(looted: Set<number>): number[] {
305	  return [...looted];
306	}
307	
308	/** Reads the opened/broken dungeon chest indices from a save (finite numbers only). */
309	export function readLootedChests(save: SaveData): number[] {
310	  if (!Array.isArray(save.lootedChests)) return [];
311	  return save.lootedChests.filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value));
312	}
313	
314	// Every known status-effect id, as a Record keyed by EffectId so adding an
315	// effect without listing it here is a compile error (keeps the reader total).
316	const VALID_EFFECT_IDS: Record<EffectId, true> = {
317	  speed: true,
318	  strength: true,
319	  regeneration: true,
320	  fire_resistance: true,
321	  water_breathing: true,
322	  poison: true
323	};
324	
325	/** Snapshots the active status effects (id + remaining seconds) for persistence. */

teach\n  `canEnchant` to honor `def.itemIds` when present; 6 readers mirroring `sharpnessBonus`.\n- `lib/game/engine/systems/statusEffects.ts` — 3 `EFFECT_ORDER` entries + 3 readers\n  (`hasteMultiplier`/`resistanceMultiplier`/`jumpBoostBonus`); no new tick (all gates/multipliers).\n- Seam wirings — one line each: `combat.ts` (Power/Punch/Knockback — Knockback adds a param to\n  `tryAttackMob`), `mobLoot.ts` `rollMobDrops` (optional `lootingLevel=0`), `items.ts`\n  `rollBlockDrops` (optional `fortuneLevel=0`, gated to an ore-block set), `playerMotion.ts`\n  (Feather Falling multiply + Jump Boost add), `mining.ts:124` (Haste), `playerLife.ts` (Resistance).\n  Optional params default to 0 so existing callers/tests stay green.\n- `lib/game/items.ts` — 3 potion `ItemDef`s (`kind:\"material\"`, `effect:{id,durationSeconds}`).\n- `lib/game/recipes.ts` — 3 `station:\"brewing\"` recipes.\n- `lib/game/save.ts` — add the 6 enchant ids to `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS` and the 3 effect ids to\n  `VALID_EFFECT_IDS` (compile-forced; **no version bump**, `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8).\n- HUD — `lib/game/render/hudPixels.ts` (3 `HudIconName` + grids + `renderHudIconPixels` cases)\n  and `components/ActiveEffects.tsx` (`EFFECT_ICONS` + `EFFECT_LABELS`).\n\n### New config tunables (`lib/game/config.ts`)\n\n```\n// Enchantments (PR1) — each maxLevel reuses ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL (3)\nPOWER_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL = 2\nPUNCH_KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.25\nKNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.4\nLOOTING_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1\nFORTUNE_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1\nFEATHER_FALLING_REDUCE_PER_LEVEL = 0.15   // cap ~0.8\n\n// Effects (PR2)\nEFFECT_HASTE_DURATION = 180;       EFFECT_HASTE_MULTIPLIER = 1.4\nEFFECT_RESISTANCE_DURATION = 120;  EFFECT_RESISTANCE_MULTIPLIER = 0.8\nEFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_DURATION = 180;  EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_VELOCITY = 2.0\n```\n\n### Docs (ship with the change — repo rule)\n\n- `docs/reference.md` — Enchantments, Status effects, and Brewing/potions tables.\n- `docs/tuning.md` — the new constants and their effect.\n- `docs/save-format.md` — note that new enchant/effect ids are additive enum values on\n  already-validated fields; v12 unchanged, `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8.\n- `docs/manual.md` — player-facing: the new enchantments and how to get the new potions.\n- `docs/adding-content.md` — add the `itemIds` allow-list note under the enchantment section\n  (the one genuinely new registry capability).\n- `CHANGELOG.md` — one `### Added` bullet per PR under `## [Unreleased]`, stating zero new\n  assets, no worldgen change, and no save-format bump.\n\n## Verification\n\n- `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run format:check && bun test && bun run build` — the\n  full CI `verify` gate must be green before anything lands on `main`. Typecheck is the primary\n  safety net here: missing entries in the totality `Record`s and exhaustive maps are compile errors.\n- New/extended tests:\n  - `lib/game/enchantments.test.ts` — the 6 new readers return flat per-level values (0 on wrong\n    gear); `canEnchant` offers Power/Punch on bow but **refuses on swords** (the `itemIds` path),\n    Knockback/Looting on weapons, Fortune on tools only.\n  - `lib/game/mobLoot.test.ts` — `rollMobDrops` with deterministic rng: Looting raises counts.\n  - `lib/game/items.test.ts` (or `mining.test.ts`) — Fortune multiplies an **ore** drop but **not**\n    a non-ore (grass/leaves/wheat).\n  - `engine/systems/combat.test.ts` — bow damage includes Power; melee knockback scales with Knockback.\n  - `playerStats.test.ts` / `GameEngine.test.ts` — Feather Falling reduces fall damage (no boots →\n    unchanged); Jump Boost raises jump velocity; Haste raises mining progress; Resistance reduces\n    armor-mitigated combat damage but **not** poison/void (assert the scoping).\n  - `lib/game/save.test.ts` — round-trip a new enchant id and a new effect id; unknown ids dropped;\n    levels clamped to maxLevel (proves backward/forward compatibility without a bump).\n  - `lib/game/recipes.test.ts` — the 3 new brewing recipes' slot ids exist and each potion's\n    `effect.id` is a valid `EffectId`.\n- Manual gameplay pass only needed if HUD icon rendering looks off (visual-appearance change);\n  no pointer-lock or renderer behavior changes otherwise.\n\n## Sequencing\n\nPR 1 and PR 2 are independent — ship in either order. Within each: registry + readers first\n(compile-driven), then the one-line seam wirings, then UI/docs/tests. Each is its own\n`<type>/<topic>` branch off `main` per the repo's branching policy (a new feature spanning\nseveral systems), Conventional Commit with a body explaining the why.\n"

> AGENT

The plan is written and grounded in the actual seams. Here's the summary before you approve.

**Recommendation: "Deepen progression"** — expand the just-shipped enchanting and potion systems, since both were built on a one-reader-per-seam pattern that's deeper-capable than its current content. I verified every seam by reading the code.

**Two independent, low-risk PRs:**
- **PR 1 — 6 enchantments:** Power, Punch, Knockback, Looting, Fortune, Feather Falling — each a single seam wiring.
- **PR 2 — 3 potions/effects:** Haste, Resistance, Jump Boost — engine-only, HUD icons, no renderer work.

**Key wins that make this safe:**
- **No save-format bump** — new ids ride the existing `enchantments`/`effects` fields, which are already validated by compile-enforced totality maps (the exact Mending precedent). `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8.
- **No new binary assets, no worldgen impact** — everything follows existing data-table + one-seam patterns.
- One genuinely new bit: an optional `itemIds` allow-list on `EnchantmentDef` so Power/Punch bind to the bow (which is `kind:"weapon", attack:0`) without appearing on swords.

**Deliberately deferred** (called out in the plan, not built): Fire Aspect/Flame, Thorns, Night Vision/Invisibility, and splash/lingering potions + negative effects — each needs a new mechanic (mob status, damage-source threading, or renderer/projectile work) and deserves its own slice.

**no version bump**, `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8).\n- HUD — `lib/game/render/hudPixels.ts` (3 `HudIconName` + grids + `renderHudIconPixels` cases)\n  and `components/ActiveEffects.tsx` (`EFFECT_ICONS` + `EFFECT_LABELS`).\n\n### New config tunables (`lib/game/config.ts`)\n\n```\n// Enchantments (PR1) — each maxLevel reuses ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL (3)\nPOWER_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL = 2\nPUNCH_KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.25\nKNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.4\nLOOTING_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1\nFORTUNE_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1\nFEATHER_FALLING_REDUCE_PER_LEVEL = 0.15   // cap ~0.8\n\n// Effects (PR2)\nEFFECT_HASTE_DURATION = 180;       EFFECT_HASTE_MULTIPLIER = 1.4\nEFFECT_RESISTANCE_DURATION = 120;  EFFECT_RESISTANCE_MULTIPLIER = 0.8\nEFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_DURATION = 180;  EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_VELOCITY = 2.0\n```\n\n### Docs (ship with the change — repo rule)\n\n- `docs/reference.md` — Enchantments, Status effects, and Brewing/potions tables.\n- `docs/tuning.md` — the new constants and their effect.\n- `docs/save-format.md` — note that new enchant/effect ids are additive enum values on\n  already-validated fields; v12 unchanged, `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8.\n- `docs/manual.md` — player-facing: the new enchantments and how to get the new potions.\n- `docs/adding-content.md` — add the `itemIds` allow-list note under the enchantment section\n  (the one genuinely new registry capability).\n- `CHANGELOG.md` — one `### Added` bullet per PR under `## [Unreleased]`, stating zero new\n  assets, no worldgen change, and no save-format bump.\n\n## Verification\n\n- `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run format:check && bun test && bun run build` — the\n  full CI `verify` gate must be green before anything lands on `main`. Within each: registry + readers first\n(compile-driven), then the one-line seam wirings, then UI/docs/tests. ## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Deepen Progression — More Enchantments & Effects

## Context

Monecraft (v0.10.0 + unreleased armor work, save v12) is a mature survival voxel game.
It just shipped the XP/enchanting system (Sharpness, Protection, Efficiency, Unbreaking,
Mending) and a 6-effect status/potion system. Both were built on a deliberate
"one-reader-per-seam" extension pattern, but each currently has only a handful of entries —
the systems are deeper-capable than their current content uses.

The user chose **"Deepen progression"** as the next direction: expand these two systems with
more enchantments and more potions/effects, capitalizing on infrastructure that already
exists. The goal is a coherent, shippable slice that adds meaningful depth using pure
one-seam additions — **no worldgen impact, no save-format bump, zero new binary assets**.

I verified by reading the code that:
- Each enchantment is a flat per-level modifier read at exactly one seam
  (`lib/game/enchantments.ts`); the `enchant` command and `EnchantingColumn` UI are generic
  over `EnchantmentId`, so a new union member surfaces automatically.
- Each effect is read at one seam; `EffectId` + `EFFECT_ORDER` + `drinkPotion` are likewise generic.
- `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS` / `VALID_EFFECT_IDS` (`lib/game/save.ts:243,316`) are
  `Record<…, true>` totality maps — a TS error forces listing new ids; `restoreEnchantments`
  drops unknown ids and clamps levels. **New ids ride the existing `enchantments` / `effects`
  fields → no save-version bump** (the exact Mending precedent).
- The bow is `kind:"weapon", attack:0` (`items.ts:245`), so bow-only enchants need a new
  optional `itemIds?: string[]` allow-list on `EnchantmentDef`, or they'd wrongly offer on swords.

## Scope

**One branch (`feat/deepen-progression`) → one PR, several focused commits.** Both halves are
the same theme and are independent (no save-format coupling forces a split), so they land as one
reviewable unit per the repo's branching policy. Each commit is a logical slice that keeps
`lint`/`typecheck`/`test` green (bisectable) and ships its own docs + tests + CHANGELOG bullet.

### Enchantments (6)

| Enchant | Seam (file) | New reader | Gated to |
|---|---|---|---|
| Power | `tryFireBow` damage (`engine/systems/combat.ts`) | `powerBonus(bow)` | bow only (`itemIds:["bow"]`) |
| Punch | `tryFireBow` knockback (`combat.ts`) | `punchKnockback(bow)` | bow only |
| Knockback | `tryAttackMob` impulse (`combat.ts`) | `knockbackBonus(weapon)` | weapons (no-op on bow) |
| Looting | `rollMobDrops` count (`lib/game/mobLoot.ts`) | `lootingBonus(weapon)` | weapons |
| Fortune | `rollBlockDrops` ore drops only (`lib/game/items.ts`) | `fortuneBonus(tool)` | tools |
| Feather Falling | fall-damage formula (`engine/systems/playerMotion.ts`) | `featherFallingReduction(boots)` | armor (boots) |

### Potions & effects (3 positive, engine-only, no renderer)

| Effect | Seam (file) | New reader | Brew recipe (proposed reagents) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Haste | mining progress `* x` (`engine/systems/mining.ts:124`) | `hasteMultiplier(state)` | Glass Bottle + Gold Ore |
| Resistance | `applyDamageWithArmor` mitigated `* x` (`engine/systems/playerLife.ts`) | `resistanceMultiplier(state)` | Glass Bottle + Leather |
| Jump Boost | jump velocity `+ x` (`playerMotion.ts:108`) | `jumpBoostBonus(state)` | Glass Bottle + Sliver Ore |

All three are mechanically invisible → **no renderer change**, HUD icons only. Resistance
scales armor-mitigated *combat* damage only (poison/void/starvation/lava left untouched, and
that scoping is asserted in tests). Jump Boost magnitude is chosen so jump height stays under
the `-14` fall-damage threshold on flat ground.

### Deferred (called out, NOT built here)

- **Fire Aspect / Flame** — need a new mob-burning status (mobs have no effect system today).
- **Thorns** — needs the attacker threaded into the damage path (`applyDamage` has no source).
- **Night Vision / Invisibility** — renderer-touching (brightness / hide third-person model)
  plus, for Invisibility, a `mobAI` detection seam.
- **Splash / Lingering potions + negative effects (Slowness, Weakness, drinkable Poison,
  Harming)** — need a thrown-projectile-with-AoE delivery mechanic; without it the negatives
  are self-harm only. Own slice.
- **Healing/Harming (instant)** — needs a new "instant" `ItemEffect` shape + a branch in
  `drinkPotion`; cheap fast-follow, kept out to keep this slice purely additive-enum.
- **Respiration / Aqua Affinity** — low value / no existing seam to negate; skip.

## Implementation

The pattern per enchantment/effect is uniform: **extend the union → add the def/order entry →
add one seam reader → wire the single call-site → add config tunable → list the id in the
save validator map → docs + test.** Apply it across the table above. Representative seams:

- `lib/game/types.ts` — extend `EnchantmentId` (+6) and `EffectId` (+3); add optional
  `itemIds?: string[]` to `EnchantmentDef` (in `enchantments.ts`).
- `lib/game/enchantments.ts` — 6 `ENCHANTMENT_DEFS` + `ENCHANTMENT_ORDER` entries; teach
  `canEnchant` to honor `def.itemIds` when present; 6 readers mirroring `sharpnessBonus`.
- `lib/game/engine/systems/statusEffects.ts` — 3 `EFFECT_ORDER` entries + 3 readers
  (`hasteMultiplier`/`resistanceMultiplier`/`jumpBoostBonus`); no new tick (all gates/multipliers).
- Seam wirings — one line each: `combat.ts` (Power/Punch/Knockback — Knockback adds a param to
  `tryAttackMob`), `mobLoot.ts` `rollMobDrops` (optional `lootingLevel=0`), `items.ts`
  `rollBlockDrops` (optional `fortuneLevel=0`, gated to an ore-block set), `playerMotion.ts`
  (Feather Falling multiply + Jump Boost add), `mining.ts:124` (Haste), `playerLife.ts` (Resistance).
  Optional params default to 0 so existing callers/tests stay green.
- `lib/game/items.ts` — 3 potion `ItemDef`s (`kind:"material"`, `effect:{id,durationSeconds}`).
- `lib/game/recipes.ts` — 3 `station:"brewing"` recipes.
- `lib/game/save.ts` — add the 6 enchant ids to `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS` and the 3 effect ids to
  `VALID_EFFECT_IDS` (compile-forced; **no version bump**, `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8).
- HUD — `lib/game/render/hudPixels.ts` (3 `HudIconName` + grids + `renderHudIconPixels` cases)
  and `components/ActiveEffects.tsx` (`EFFECT_ICONS` + `EFFECT_LABELS`).

### New config tunables (`lib/game/config.ts`)

```
// Enchantments (PR1) — each maxLevel reuses ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL (3)
POWER_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL = 2
PUNCH_KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.25
KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL = 0.4
LOOTING_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1
FORTUNE_BONUS_PER_LEVEL = 1
FEATHER_FALLING_REDUCE_PER_LEVEL = 0.15   // cap ~0.8

// Effects (PR2)
EFFECT_HASTE_DURATION = 180;       EFFECT_HASTE_MULTIPLIER = 1.4
EFFECT_RESISTANCE_DURATION = 120;  EFFECT_RESISTANCE_MULTIPLIER = 0.8
EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_DURATION = 180;  EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_VELOCITY = 2.0
```

### Docs (ship with the change — repo rule)

- `docs/reference.md` — Enchantments, Status effects, and Brewing/potions tables.
- `docs/tuning.md` — the new constants and their effect.
- `docs/save-format.md` — note that new enchant/effect ids are additive enum values on
  already-validated fields; v12 unchanged, `WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8.
- `docs/manual.md` — player-facing: the new enchantments and how to get the new potions.
- `docs/adding-content.md` — add the `itemIds` allow-list note under the enchantment section
  (the one genuinely new registry capability).
- `CHANGELOG.md` — `### Added` bullets under `## [Unreleased]` (appended per commit), stating
  zero new assets, no worldgen change, and no save-format bump.

## Verification

- `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run format:check && bun test && bun run build` — the
  full CI `verify` gate must be green before anything lands on `main`. Typecheck is the primary
  safety net here: missing entries in the totality `Record`s and exhaustive maps are compile errors.
- New/extended tests:
  - `lib/game/enchantments.test.ts` — the 6 new readers return flat per-level values (0 on wrong
    gear); `canEnchant` offers Power/Punch on bow but **refuses on swords** (the `itemIds` path),
    Knockback/Looting on weapons, Fortune on tools only.
  - `lib/game/mobLoot.test.ts` — `rollMobDrops` with deterministic rng: Looting raises counts.
  - `lib/game/items.test.ts` (or `mining.test.ts`) — Fortune multiplies an **ore** drop but **not**
    a non-ore (grass/leaves/wheat).
  - `engine/systems/combat.test.ts` — bow damage includes Power; melee knockback scales with Knockback.
  - `playerStats.test.ts` / `GameEngine.test.ts` — Feather Falling reduces fall damage (no boots →
    unchanged); Jump Boost raises jump velocity; Haste raises mining progress; Resistance reduces
    armor-mitigated combat damage but **not** poison/void (assert the scoping).
  - `lib/game/save.test.ts` — round-trip a new enchant id and a new effect id; unknown ids dropped;
    levels clamped to maxLevel (proves backward/forward compatibility without a bump).
  - `lib/game/recipes.test.ts` — the 3 new brewing recipes' slot ids exist and each potion's
    `effect.id` is a valid `EffectId`.
- Manual gameplay pass only needed if HUD icon rendering looks off (visual-appearance change);
  no pointer-lock or renderer behavior changes otherwise.

## Sequencing — one branch, focused commits

Branch `feat/deepen-progression` off `main`. Each commit below is a logical slice that stays
green (`lint`/`typecheck`/`test`) and includes its own seam wiring, config, save-validator
entry, docs, tests, and a CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]` bullet. Conventional Commit, body explains the why.

1. `feat(enchant): add Power and Punch for bows` — includes the optional `itemIds` allow-list
   on `EnchantmentDef` (the one new registry capability) since it's first needed here.
2. `feat(enchant): add Knockback and Looting for weapons` — `tryAttackMob` knockback param +
   `rollMobDrops` looting count.
3. `feat(enchant): add Fortune and Feather Falling` — ore-gated `rollBlockDrops` + the
   `playerMotion` fall-damage reducer.
4. `feat(brewing): add Haste, Resistance, and Jump Boost potions` — the 3 effects, readers,
   brewing recipes, and HUD icons together (shared wiring).

Within each commit: extend the union + add def/reader (compile-driven) first, then the one-line
seam wiring, then items/recipes/UI, then docs + tests. Run the full CI `verify` gate before
opening the PR; `bun run test:e2e` only if HUD icon rendering is touched in a way worth a smoke pass.

```
