```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import {
3	  ARROW_SPEED,
4	  ARROW_TTL,
5	  ATTACK_AIM_DOT,
6	  ATTACK_REACH,
7	  BOW_ARROW_DAMAGE,
8	  BOW_COOLDOWN_SECONDS,
9	  BOW_DURABILITY_PER_SHOT,
10	  BOW_KNOCKBACK,
11	  EYE_HEIGHT,
12	  FIST_DAMAGE
13	} from "@/lib/game/config";
14	import { adjustSlotCount, consumeToolDurability, countsById } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
15	import type { InventorySlot, MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
16	import type { EmitGameEvent, GameState } from "../state";
17	import { spawnArrow } from "../projectiles";
18	import { lookDirection } from "./playerMotion";
19	
20	const scratchForward = new THREE.Vector3();
21	const scratchOrigin = new THREE.Vector3();
22	const scratchToMob = new THREE.Vector3();
23	const scratchKnock = new THREE.Vector3();
24	
25	export function weaponDamage(state: GameState): number {
26	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
27	  if (slot?.kind === "weapon" && slot.count > 0) return slot.attack ?? 8;
28	  return FIST_DAMAGE;
29	}
30	
31	export function weaponReach(state: GameState): number {
32	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
33	  if (slot?.kind === "weapon" && slot.count > 0) return slot.meleeReach ?? ATTACK_REACH;
34	  return ATTACK_REACH;
35	}
36	
37	/**
38	 * Index of the mob nearest the crosshair within melee reach and aim cone, or
39	 * -1. Shared by attacking and by feeding animals (Phase 5) so both use the same
40	 * "what am I pointing at" rule.
41	 */
42	export function findAimedMobIndex(state: GameState, reach = ATTACK_REACH): number {
43	  const { position } = state.player;
44	  scratchOrigin.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
45	  lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchForward);
46	
47	  let bestIndex = -1;
48	  let bestDist = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
49	
50	  for (let i = 0; i < state.mobs.length; i += 1) {
51	    const mob = state.mobs[i];
52	    scratchToMob.copy(mob.position).sub(scratchOrigin);
53	    const dist = scratchToMob.length();
54	    if (dist > reach) continue;
55	    scratchToMob.normalize();
56	    if (scratchForward.dot(scratchToMob) < ATTACK_AIM_DOT) continue;
57	    if (dist < bestDist) {
58	      bestDist = dist;
59	      bestIndex = i;
60	    }
61	  }
62	  return bestIndex;
63	}
64	
65	/**
66	 * Melee attack at the mob nearest the crosshair within reach. Returns the kind
67	 * of the mob hit (or null); the caller (engine) handles death drops and durability.
68	 */
69	export function tryAttackMob(state: GameState, damage: number, onMobKilled: (index: number) => void, reach = ATTACK_REACH): MobKind | null {
70	  const { position } = state.player;
71	  const bestIndex = findAimedMobIndex(state, reach);
72	  if (bestIndex < 0) return null;
73	  const mob = state.mobs[bestIndex];
74	  mob.hp -= damage;
75	
76	  scratchKnock.copy(mob.position).sub(position).setY(0);
77	  if (scratchKnock.lengthSq() > 0.0001) {
78	    scratchKnock.normalize();
79	    mob.direction.copy(scratchKnock);
80	    mob.position.addScaledVector(scratchKnock, 0.75);
81	    mob.position.y += 0.12;
82	  }
83	
84	  if (mob.hp <= 0) onMobKilled(bestIndex);
85	  return mob.kind;
86	}
87	
88	/** True when the held slot is a usable bow (the attack input fires instead of melees). */
89	export function isBow(slot: InventorySlot | undefined): boolean {
90	  return slot?.id === "bow" && slot.count > 0;
91	}
92	
93	/**
94	 * Fires one arrow from the player's eye along their look direction, consuming an
95	 * arrow and a point of bow durability and arming the fire-rate cooldown. Returns
96	 * false (no shot) when the bow is on cooldown or the player has no arrows — the
97	 * caller has already confirmed a bow is held via isBow, so a bow never melees.
98	 */
99	export function tryFireBow(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng?: () => number): boolean {
100	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
101	  if (!isBow(slot)) return false;
102	  if (state.timers.bowCooldownTimer > 0) return false;
103	  if ((countsById(state.inventory).get("arrow") ?? 0) < 1) return false;
104	
105	  const { position, yaw, pitch } = state.player;
106	  scratchOrigin.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
107	  lookDirection(yaw, pitch, scratchForward);
108	  spawnArrow(state, scratchOrigin.x, scratchOrigin.y, scratchOrigin.z, scratchForward, {
109	    speed: ARROW_SPEED,
110	    damage: BOW_ARROW_DAMAGE,
111	    knockback: BOW_KNOCKBACK,
112	    fromPlayer: true,
113	    ttl: ARROW_TTL
114	  });
115	
116	  state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, "arrow", -1) ?? state.inventory;
117	  state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, BOW_DURABILITY_PER_SHOT, rng) ?? state.inventory;
118	  state.timers.bowCooldownTimer = BOW_COOLDOWN_SECONDS;
119	  emit({ type: "bowFired" });
120	  return true;
121	}
122
```

```
127	/**
128	 * v4 save shape: v3 plus chest contents as block-entities. The field is
129	 * optional so the v3→v4 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v4 saves load
130	 * with no containers.
131	 */
132	export type SaveDataV4 = Omit<SaveDataV3, "version"> & {
133	  version: 4;
134	  blockEntities?: SavedContainer[];
135	};
136	
137	/**
138	 * v5 save shape: v4 plus the set of dungeon loot-chest voxel indices the player
139	 * has already opened or broken. Dungeon chests are filled lazily on first access
140	 * and this set — not the chest's emptiness — is what prevents a re-roll on reload
141	 * (an emptied chest is dropped from `blockEntities`). The field is optional so
142	 * the v4→v5 migration is a pure version bump.
143	 */
144	export type SaveDataV5 = Omit<SaveDataV4, "version"> & {
145	  version: 5;
146	  lootedChests?: number[];
147	  /** Generation preset; absent ⇒ "default" (pre-feature and legacy saves). Like `seed`, fixed for the world's life. */
148	  worldType?: WorldType;
149	};
150	
151	/** One persisted status effect: its id and the seconds remaining when saved. */
152	export type SavedEffect = { id: EffectId; remaining: number };
153	
154	/**
155	 * v6 save shape: v5 plus the active status effects (with their remaining
156	 * seconds). The field is optional so the v5→v6 migration is a pure version bump
157	 * and pre-effect saves load with none. Effects are cleared on death, so a
158	 * saved-then-reloaded world restores whatever was active at save.
159	 */
160	export type SaveDataV6 = Omit<SaveDataV5, "version"> & {
161	  version: 6;
162	  effects?: SavedEffect[];
163	};
164	
165	/**
166	 * v7 save shape: v6 plus banked XP (`xp`). Enchantments ride inside each
167	 * `SavedSlot` (additive). Both are optional, so the v6→v7 migration is a pure
168	 * version bump and pre-XP saves load with `xp` 0 and no enchantments. Unlike
169	 * effects, XP is a long-term currency and is NOT cleared on death.
170	 */
171	export type SaveDataV7 = Omit<SaveDataV6, "version"> & {
172	  version: 7;
173	  xp?: number;
174	};
175	
176	/**
177	 * v8 save shape: v7 plus the player's game mode (`gameMode`). The field is
178	 * optional, so the v7→v8 migration is a pure version bump and pre-mode saves
179	 * load as "survival". Unlike `worldType` (fixed for the world's life),
180	 * `gameMode` is switchable in-game, so the saved value is the *current* mode.
181	 */
182	export type SaveDataV8 = Omit<SaveDataV7, "version"> & {
183	  version: 8;
184	  gameMode?: GameMode;
185	};
186	
187	/**
188	 * v9 save shape: v8 plus the player's difficulty (`difficulty`). The field is
189	 * optional, so the v8→v9 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v9 saves load
190	 * as "normal". Like `gameMode`, difficulty is switchable in-game, so the saved
191	 * value is the *current* difficulty.
192	 */
193	export type SaveDataV9 = Omit<SaveDataV8, "version"> & {
194	  version: 9;
195	  difficulty?: Difficulty;
196	};
197	
198	/**
199	 * v10 save shape: v9 plus the Hardcore flag (`hardcore`, immutable for the
200	 * world's life) and the permadeath `gameOver` flag (set once a hardcore run has
201	 * ended). Both are optional, so the v9→v10 migration is a pure version bump and
202	 * pre-v10 saves load as a normal, non-hardcore world. A persisted `gameOver`
203	 * reloads straight into the spectator "dead world" state, never a playable one.
204	 */
205	export type SaveDataV10 = Omit<SaveDataV9, "version"> & {
206	  version: 10;
207	  hardcore?: boolean;
208	  gameOver?: boolean;
209	};
210	
211	/**
212	 * v11 save shape: v10 plus a per-item `customName` (the anvil rename), which rides
213	 * inside each `SavedSlot` (additive). The Mending enchantment rides the existing
214	 * per-slot `enchantments`, so it adds no new field. The v10→v11 migration is a
215	 * pure version bump and pre-v11 saves load with no custom names.
216	 */
217	export type SaveDataV11 = Omit<SaveDataV10, "version"> & {
218	  version: 11;
219	};
220	
221	/**
222	 * Current save shape (v12): armor moves to dedicated storage. `equippedArmor` is no
223	 * longer a by-id reference into the inventory (`SavedArmorById`) but the worn pieces
224	 * themselves (`SavedEquippedArmor`, per-slot `SavedSlot`). The v11→v12 migration
225	 * moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record so a
226	 * worn piece no longer double-occupies an inventory slot.
227	 */
228	export type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV11, "version" | "equippedArmor"> & {
229	  version: 12;
230	  equippedArmor?: SavedEquippedArmor;
231	};
232	
```

```
259	
260	export const ITEM_DEF_BY_ID: Record<string, ItemDef> = Object.fromEntries(ITEM_DEFS.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
261	
262	export function maxStackSizeForItem(itemId: string): number {
263	  return ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId]?.maxDurability ? 1 : MAX_STACK_SIZE;
264	}
265	
266	export function createEmptySlot(): InventorySlot {
267	  return { id: null, label: "Empty", kind: null, count: 0 };
268	}
269	
270	export function createSlot(itemId: string, count: number): InventorySlot {
271	  const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId];
272	  if (!def) return createEmptySlot();
273	  const slot: InventorySlot = { ...def, count: Math.min(maxStackSizeForItem(itemId), Math.max(0, Math.floor(count))) };
274	  if ((def.kind === "tool" || def.kind === "weapon" || def.kind === "armor") && def.maxDurability) {
275	    slot.maxDurability = def.maxDurability;
276	    slot.durability = def.maxDurability;
277	  }
278	  return slot;
279	}
280	
281	/** The name to show for a slot — its player-set anvil name if any, else the item label. */
282	export function displayName(slot: InventorySlot): string {
283	  return slot.customName ?? slot.label;
284	}
285	
286	/**
287	 * The material a piece of durable gear is repaired with at the anvil — its
288	 * dominant crafting-tier ingredient. Gear absent from this map (none today) can
289	 * still be combined with a duplicate; it just has no material-repair option.
290	 */
291	export const REPAIR_MATERIAL_BY_ITEM: Record<string, string> = {
292	  wood_pickaxe: "planks",
293	  wood_sword: "planks",
294	  wood_spear: "planks",
295	  wood_hoe: "planks",
296	  stone_pickaxe: "cobble",

303	  ruby_pickaxe: "ruby_ore",
304	  ruby_sword: "ruby_ore",

309	  sapphire_pickaxe: "sapphire_ore",
310	  sapphire_sword: "sapphire_ore",

322	  chestplate: "gold_ore",

326	
327	export function createInitialInventory(): InventorySlot[] {
328	  const slots: InventorySlot[] = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
329	  const starter: Array<{ id: string; count: number }> = [
330	    { id: "grass", count: 64 },
331	    { id: "dirt", count: 64 },
332	    { id: "stone", count: 64 },
333	    { id: "wood", count: 64 },
334	    { id: "planks", count: 20 },
335	    { id: "cobble", count: 20 },
336	    { id: "sand", count: 20 },
337	    { id: "wood_pickaxe", count: 1 },
338	    { id: "knife", count: 1 }
339	  ];
340	  for (let i = 0; i < starter.length && i < slots.length; i += 1) slots[i] = createSlot(starter[i].id, starter[i].count);
341	  return slots;
342	}
343	
344	export const BLOCK_TO_SLOT: Partial<Record<BlockId, string>> = {
345	  [BlockId.Grass]: "grass",
346	  [BlockId.Dirt]: "dirt",
347	  [BlockId.Stone]: "stone",
348	  [BlockId.Wood]: "wood",
349	  // Leaves drop only a sapling (by chance) — handled in rollBlockDrops, not here.
350	  [BlockId.Planks]: "planks",
351	  [BlockId.Cobblestone]: "cobble",
352	  [BlockId.Sand]: "sand",
353	  [BlockId.Brick]: "brick",
354	  [BlockId.Glass]: "glass",
355	  // Coal ore drops the usable coal item directly (not a placeable ore block).
356	  [BlockId.CoalOre]: "coal",
357	  [BlockId.SliverOre]: "sliver_ore",
358	  [BlockId.RubyOre]: "ruby_ore",
359	  [BlockId.GoldOre]: "gold_ore",
360	  [BlockId.SapphireOre]: "sapphire_ore",
361	  [BlockId.DiamondOre]: "diamond_ore",
362	  [BlockId.Snow]: "snow",
363	  [BlockId.Cactus]: "cactus",
364	  [BlockId.Bed]: "bed",
365	  [BlockId.Furnace]: "furnace",
366	  [BlockId.Chest]: "chest",
367	  [BlockId.BrewingStand]: "brewing_stand",
368	  [BlockId.EnchantingTable]: "enchanting_table",
369	  [BlockId.Anvil]: "anvil",
370	  [BlockId.Grindstone]: "grindstone",
371	  [BlockId.MossyCobblestone]: "mossy_cobble",
372	  [BlockId.Torch]: "torch",
373	  [BlockId.Tnt]: "tnt",
374	  [BlockId.Sapling]: "sapling",
375	  [BlockId.DoorNorthLower]: "door",
376	  // Tilled soil reverts to dirt; immature wheat returns its seed.
377	  [BlockId.Farmland]: "dirt",
378	  [BlockId.WheatStage0]: "seeds",
379	  [BlockId.WheatStage1]: "seeds",
380	  [BlockId.WheatStage2]: "seeds"
381	};
382	
383	/**
384	 * Items a broken block yields. The default is its single `BLOCK_TO_SLOT` entry;
385	 * grass occasionally also drops a seed (the natural seed source), mature wheat
386	 * drops wheat plus 1–2 seeds, and leaves occasionally drop a sapling (their only
387	 * yield — the renewable tree source). `rng` is injectable for deterministic tests.
388	 */
389	export function rollBlockDrops(block: BlockId, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
390	  if (isDoorBlock(block)) return [{ itemId: "door", count: 1 }];
391	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
392	  const base = BLOCK_TO_SLOT[block];
393	  if (base) drops.push({ itemId: base, count: 1 });
394	
395	  if (block === BlockId.Leaves && rng() < LEAVES_SAPLING_DROP_CHANCE) {
396	    drops.push({ itemId: "sapling", count: 1 });
397	  }
398	  if (block === BlockId.Grass && rng() < GRASS_SEED_DROP_CHANCE) {
399	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 });
400	  }
401	  if (block === BlockId.WheatStage3) {
402	    drops.push({ itemId: "wheat", count: 1 });
403	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 + Math.floor(rng() * 2) });
404	  }
405	  return drops;
406	}
407
```

```
1	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
2	
3	/**
4	 * A single loot entry: yields a uniformly random count in [min, max], gated by
5	 * an optional drop chance (default: always). Counts are inclusive on both ends.
6	 */
7	export type MobDrop = {
8	  itemId: string;
9	  min: number;
10	  max: number;
11	  /** 0..1 probability the entry drops at all; omitted means always. */
12	  chance?: number;
13	};
14	
15	/**
16	 * What each mob drops when it dies. Hostiles yield crafting/combat materials,
17	 * passives yield materials plus raw meat. Item ids must exist in ITEM_DEFS —
18	 * mobLoot.test.ts enforces that.
19	 */
20	export const MOB_DROPS: Record<MobKind, MobDrop[]> = {
21	  sheep: [
22	    { itemId: "wool", min: 1, max: 2 },
23	    { itemId: "raw_mutton", min: 1, max: 1 }
24	  ],
25	  chicken: [
26	    { itemId: "feather", min: 0, max: 2 },
27	    { itemId: "raw_chicken", min: 1, max: 1 }
28	  ],
29	  horse: [{ itemId: "leather", min: 1, max: 2 }],
30	  cow: [
31	    { itemId: "leather", min: 1, max: 2 },
32	    { itemId: "raw_beef", min: 1, max: 1 }
33	  ],
34	  pig: [{ itemId: "raw_porkchop", min: 1, max: 1 }],
35	  zombie: [{ itemId: "rotten_flesh", min: 1, max: 2 }],
36	  skeleton: [{ itemId: "bone", min: 1, max: 2 }],
37	  spider: [{ itemId: "string", min: 0, max: 2 }],
38	  creeper: [{ itemId: "gunpowder", min: 1, max: 2 }],
39	  // Villagers are non-combat trade NPCs — killing one yields nothing (don't murder
40	  // your shopkeeper). The empty table is intentional; mobLoot.test.ts allows it.
41	  villager: [],
42	  boss: [
43	    { itemId: "dragon_heart", min: 1, max: 1 },
44	    { itemId: "diamond_ore", min: 2, max: 4 }
45	  ]
46	};
47	
48	/** Clamps an rng sample into [0, 1) so a pathological injected rng can't over-roll. */
49	const clampUnit = (v: number): number => Math.min(1 - Number.EPSILON, Math.max(0, v));
50	
51	/**
52	 * Rolls the drop table for a mob kind. Returns one entry per item that yielded a
53	 * positive count; the engine adds each to the inventory. `rng` is injectable so
54	 * tests get deterministic counts (0 → min, ~1 → max).
55	 */
56	export function rollMobDrops(kind: MobKind, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
57	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
58	  for (const entry of MOB_DROPS[kind]) {
59	    if (entry.chance !== undefined && clampUnit(rng()) >= entry.chance) continue;
60	    const span = entry.max - entry.min + 1;
61	    const count = entry.min + Math.floor(clampUnit(rng()) * span);
62	    if (count > 0) drops.push({ itemId: entry.itemId, count });
63	  }
64	  return drops;
65	}
66
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId, collidesAt, doorBlock, doorFacingFromYaw, doorState, isDoorBlock, voxelRaycast } from "@/lib/world";
3	import { BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER, CHEST_SLOTS, EYE_HEIGHT, MINE_REACH, MINING_RATE, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT } from "@/lib/game/config";
4	import { BREAK_HARDNESS, createEmptySlot, rollBlockDrops } from "@/lib/game/items";
5	import { adjustSlotCount, consumeToolDurability, tryInsertSlots } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
6	import { canEditBlocks, freeBuild } from "@/lib/game/gameModes";
7	import type { EmitGameEvent, FrameInput, GameState } from "../state";
8	import { efficiencyMultiplier } from "@/lib/game/enchantments";
9	import { fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted } from "./dungeon";
10	import { lookDirection } from "./playerMotion";
11	import { awardXp, xpForBlock } from "./xp";
12	import type { InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";

14	const scratchEye = new THREE.Vector3();
15	const scratchDir = new THREE.Vector3();

17	function eyePosition(state: GameState, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
18	  const { position } = state.player;
19	  return out.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
20	}
21	
22	export function selectedTool(state: GameState): InventorySlot | null {
23	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
24	  return slot?.kind === "tool" && slot.count > 0 ? slot : null;
25	}
26	
27	export function canMineBlock(block: BlockId, toolTier: number): boolean {
28	  if (block === BlockId.Stone || block === BlockId.Cobblestone || block === BlockId.Brick) return toolTier >= 1;
29	  if (block === BlockId.CoalOre) return toolTier >= 1;
30	  if (block === BlockId.SliverOre) return toolTier >= 2;
31	  if (block === BlockId.RubyOre) return toolTier >= 3;
32	  if (block === BlockId.GoldOre) return toolTier >= 3;
33	  if (block === BlockId.SapphireOre) return toolTier >= 4;
34	  if (block === BlockId.DiamondOre) return toolTier >= 4;
35	  return true;
36	}
37	
38	export function miningSpeed(tool: InventorySlot | null): number {
39	  return (tool?.minePower ?? BARE_HAND_MINE_POWER) * efficiencyMultiplier(tool);
40	}
41	
42	export function resetMining(state: GameState): void {
43	  state.mining.targetKey = "";
44	  state.mining.progress = 0;
45	}
46	
47	function addBlockDrop(state: GameState, block: BlockId, rng: () => number): void {
48	  for (const drop of rollBlockDrops(block, rng)) {
49	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
50	  }
51	}
52	
53	/**
54	 * Empties a broken chest into the inventory. Returns true if the break may
55	 * proceed (the chest was empty, or its contents all fit), false to refuse it
56	 * (no room — the chest is left intact). On success the container entry is
57	 * removed and a pickedUp toast announces what was retrieved.
58	 */
59	function spillChestOnBreak(state: GameState, idx: number, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
60	  // Breaking an unopened dungeon chest still pays out its loot.
61	  fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted(state, idx);
62	  const container = state.containers.get(idx);
63	  const items = container?.filter((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0) ?? [];
64	  if (items.length > 0) {
65	    const merged = tryInsertSlots(state.inventory, items);
66	    if (!merged) {
67	      emit({ type: "breakBlocked", reason: "containerFull" });
68	      return false;
69	    }
70	    state.inventory = merged;
71	    const picked = new Map<string, number>();
72	    for (const slot of items) picked.set(slot.id!, (picked.get(slot.id!) ?? 0) + slot.count);
73	    emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: [...picked].map(([itemId, count]) => ({ itemId, count })) });
74	  }
75	  state.containers.delete(idx);
76	  return true;
77	}
78	
79	/** Advances mining progress while the mouse is held; breaks the block at full progress. */
80	export function tickMining(state: GameState, input: FrameInput, dt: number, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng: () => number): void {
81	  if (!input.leftMouseHeld) {
82	    // Releasing the button abandons progress (matching the crack overlay).
83	    if (state.mining.progress > 0) resetMining(state);
84	    return;
85	  }
86	  if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || !input.pointerLocked) return;
87	  // Adventure and Spectator can't break terrain.
88	  if (!canEditBlocks(state.gameMode)) {
89	    resetMining(state);
90	    return;
91	  }
92	
93	  const { world, mining } = state;
94	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
95	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
96	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
97	  if (!result) {
98	    resetMining(state);
99	    return;
100	  }
101	
102	  const bx = result.hit.x;
103	  const by = result.hit.y;
104	  const bz = result.hit.z;
105	  const targetBlock = world.get(bx, by, bz);
106	  const tool = selectedTool(state);
107	  const tier = tool?.mineTier ?? 0;
108	  // Creative breaks anything (bar bedrock) instantly, regardless of tool tier.
109	  const creative = freeBuild(state.gameMode);
110	
111	  if (targetBlock === BlockId.Bedrock || targetBlock === BlockId.Air || (!creative && !canMineBlock(targetBlock as BlockId, tier))) {
112	    resetMining(state);
113	    return;
114	  }
115	
116	  const key = `${bx},${by},${bz}`;
117	  if (mining.targetKey !== key) {
118	    mining.targetKey = key;
119	    mining.progress = 0;
120	  }
121	
122	  if (!creative) {
123	    const hardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[targetBlock as BlockId] ?? 2;
124	    mining.progress += dt * miningSpeed(tool) * MINING_RATE;
125	    if (mining.progress < hardness) return;
126	  }
127	
128	  // Breaking a chest empties it into the inventory first; if it does not all
129	  // fit, refuse the break so nothing is lost (the chest stays intact). Creative
130	  // breaks for free — the chest and its contents just vanish (no spill, no refusal).
131	  const chestIndex = targetBlock === BlockId.Chest ? world.index(bx, by, bz) : null;
132	  if (chestIndex !== null) {
133	    if (creative) state.containers.delete(chestIndex);
134	    else if (!spillChestOnBreak(state, chestIndex, emit)) {
135	      resetMining(state);
136	      return;
137	    }
138	  }
139	
140	  if (isDoorBlock(targetBlock)) {
141	    const door = doorState(targetBlock)!;
142	    const other = doorState(world.get(bx, door.upper ? by - 1 : by + 1, bz));
143	    state.blockChanges.set(bx, by, bz, BlockId.Air);
144	    if (other && other.upper !== door.upper) {
145	      state.blockChanges.set(bx, door.upper ? by - 1 : by + 1, bz, BlockId.Air);
146	    }
147	  } else {
148	    state.blockChanges.set(bx, by, bz, BlockId.Air);
149	  }
150	  // Creative breaks for free: no tool wear, no drops, no XP.
151	  if (tool && !creative) state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1, rng) ?? state.inventory;
152	  if (!creative) {
153	    addBlockDrop(state, targetBlock as BlockId, rng);
154	    awardXp(state, xpForBlock(targetBlock as BlockId), emit); // ore blocks grant XP; everything else is 0
155	  }
156	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
157	  resetMining(state);
158	  emit({ type: "blockBroken", blockId: targetBlock as BlockId, x: bx, y: by, z: bz });
159	}
160	
161	/** Places the selected block against the targeted face, refusing self-entombment. */
162	export function placeSelectedBlock(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
163	  if (!canEditBlocks(state.gameMode)) return; // Adventure and Spectator can't place
164	  const { world } = state;
165	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
166	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
167	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
168	  if (!result) return;
169	
170	  const tx = result.previous.x;
171	  const ty = result.previous.y;
172	  const tz = result.previous.z;
173	  if (!world.inBounds(tx, ty, tz)) return;
174	  const replacedBlock = world.get(tx, ty, tz);
175	  if (replacedBlock !== BlockId.Air && replacedBlock !== BlockId.Water) return;
176	
177	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
178	  if (!slot || !slot.id || slot.kind !== "block" || slot.count <= 0 || slot.blockId === undefined) return;
179	  if (slot.blockId === BlockId.Bedrock) return;
180	
181	  // Creative builds without spending the held stack (so no take, no refund).
182	  const consume = !freeBuild(state.gameMode);
183	  if (consume) {
184	    const afterTake = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
185	    if (!afterTake) return;
186	    state.inventory = afterTake;
187	  }
188	  let replacedUpper: BlockId | null = null;
189	  if (slot.id === "door") {
190	    const support = world.get(tx, ty - 1, tz);
191	    replacedUpper = world.get(tx, ty + 1, tz) as BlockId;
192	    if (ty + 1 >= world.sizeY || (replacedUpper !== BlockId.Air && replacedUpper !== BlockId.Water) || !world.isSolid(tx, ty - 1, tz) || isDoorBlock(support)) {
193	      if (consume) state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, 1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
194	      return;
195	    }
196	    const facing = doorFacingFromYaw(state.player.yaw);
197	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, doorBlock(facing, false, false));
198	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty + 1, tz, doorBlock(facing, false, true));
199	  } else {
200	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, slot.blockId);
201	  }
202	  if (collidesAt(world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) {
203	    state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty, tz, replacedBlock as BlockId);
204	    if (replacedUpper !== null) state.blockChanges.set(tx, ty + 1, tz, replacedUpper);
205	    if (consume) state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, 1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
206	    return;
207	  }
208	
209	  // A placed chest gets a fresh, empty block-entity store.
210	  if (slot.blockId === BlockId.Chest) {
211	    state.containers.set(
212	      world.index(tx, ty, tz),
213	      Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot())
214	    );
215	  }
216	
217	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
218	  emit({ type: "blockPlaced", blockId: slot.blockId, x: tx, y: ty, z: tz });
219	}
220
```

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { collidesAt, hasSupportUnderPlayer } from "@/lib/world";
3	import { canFly, isNoclip } from "@/lib/game/gameModes";
4	import {
5	  CROUCH_SPEED,
6	  FLY_SPEED,
7	  GRAVITY,
8	  JUMP_VELOCITY,
9	  PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH,
10	  PLAYER_HEIGHT,
11	  SPRINT_MIN_HUNGER,
12	  SPRINT_SPEED,
13	  WALK_SPEED,
14	  WORLD_BORDER_PADDING
15	} from "@/lib/game/config";
16	import type { FrameInput, GameState } from "../state";
17	import { speedScaleFromHunger } from "./playerStats";
18	import { speedMultiplier } from "./statusEffects";
19	
20	export type MoveTickResult = {
21	  didSprint: boolean;
22	  didWalk: boolean;
23	  didJump: boolean;
24	  /** Touched down this tick after being airborne. */
25	  didLand: boolean;
26	  /** Downward speed at touchdown (positive); 0 when didLand is false. */
27	  landImpact: number;
28	  horizontalDistance: number;
29	};
30	
31	// Module-scope scratch vectors — the tick runs every frame and must not allocate.
32	const scratchForward = new THREE.Vector3();
33	const scratchRight = new THREE.Vector3();
34	const scratchMoveDir = new THREE.Vector3();
35	const UP = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
36	
37	/** Horizontal forward direction for a yaw angle (camera order YXZ, looking down -Z). */
38	export function forwardFromYaw(yaw: number, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
39	  return out.set(-Math.sin(yaw), 0, -Math.cos(yaw)).normalize();
40	}
41	
42	/** Full look direction for yaw+pitch — what the camera's getWorldDirection returns. */
43	export function lookDirection(yaw: number, pitch: number, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
44	  const cp = Math.cos(pitch);
45	  return out.set(-cp * Math.sin(yaw), Math.sin(pitch), -cp * Math.cos(yaw));
46	}
47	
48	export function tickPlayerMotion(state: GameState, input: FrameInput, dt: number, applyDamage: (amount: number) => void): MoveTickResult {
49	  const { world, player, timers } = state;
50	  const keys = input.keys;
51	  // Spectator phases through terrain (noclip); Creative/Spectator fly with direct
52	  // vertical control instead of gravity.
53	  const noclip = isNoclip(state.gameMode);
54	  const flying = noclip || (state.isFlying && canFly(state.gameMode));
55	
56	  const stepAxis = (axis: "x" | "y" | "z", amount: number) => {
57	    if (noclip) {
58	      player.position[axis] += amount; // pass straight through blocks
59	      return;
60	    }
61	    const stepSize = 0.05 * Math.sign(amount);
62	    let remaining = amount;
63	    while (Math.abs(remaining) > 1e-6) {
64	      const step = Math.abs(remaining) > Math.abs(stepSize) ? stepSize : remaining;
65	      player.position[axis] += step;
66	      if (collidesAt(world, player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) {
67	        player.position[axis] -= step;
68	        if (axis === "y" && step < 0) player.onGround = true;
69	        if (axis === "y") player.velocity.y = 0;
70	        break;
71	      }
72	      remaining -= step;
73	    }
74	  };
75	
76	  const forwardInput = (keys.has("KeyW") ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has("KeyS") ? 1 : 0);
77	  const strafeInput = (keys.has("KeyD") ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has("KeyA") ? 1 : 0);
78	  const wantsJump = keys.has("Space");
79	  const crouching = keys.has("KeyC");
80	
81	  forwardFromYaw(player.yaw, scratchForward);
82	  scratchRight.crossVectors(scratchForward, UP).normalize();
83	  scratchMoveDir.set(0, 0, 0);
84	  scratchMoveDir.addScaledVector(scratchForward, forwardInput);
85	  scratchMoveDir.addScaledVector(scratchRight, strafeInput);
86	  if (scratchMoveDir.lengthSq() > 0) scratchMoveDir.normalize();
87	
88	  const speedScale = speedScaleFromHunger(state.hunger);
89	  const canSprint = state.hunger > SPRINT_MIN_HUNGER;
90	  const sprinting = canSprint && forwardInput > 0 && keys.has("KeyW") && input.capsActive && !crouching;
91	  const baseSpeed = crouching ? CROUCH_SPEED : sprinting ? SPRINT_SPEED * speedScale : WALK_SPEED * speedScale;
92	  const speed = baseSpeed * speedMultiplier(state);
93	
94	  player.velocity.x = scratchMoveDir.x * speed;
95	  player.velocity.z = scratchMoveDir.z * speed;
96	
97	  const wasGrounded = player.onGround;
98	  let didJump = false;
99	  if (flying) {
100	    // Flight: direct vertical control, no gravity. Space ascends, crouch
101	    // descends, neither (or both) hovers. Collision still applies in Creative;
102	    // Spectator's noclip is handled in stepAxis.
103	    const ascend = (wantsJump ? 1 : 0) - (crouching ? 1 : 0);
104	    player.velocity.y = ascend * FLY_SPEED;
105	  } else {
106	    player.velocity.y -= GRAVITY * dt;
107	    if (wantsJump && player.onGround && !crouching) {
108	      player.velocity.y = JUMP_VELOCITY;
109	      player.onGround = false;
110	      didJump = true;
111	    }
112	  }
113	
114	  const vyBeforeMove = player.velocity.y;
115	  const startX = player.position.x;
116	  const startZ = player.position.z;
117	  const prevX = player.position.x;
118	  const prevZ = player.position.z;
119	
120	  player.onGround = false;
121	  stepAxis("x", player.velocity.x * dt);
122	  stepAxis("z", player.velocity.z * dt);
123	  stepAxis("y", player.velocity.y * dt);
124	
125	  // Depenetration: if still colliding after movement, nudge up (skipped in noclip).
126	  if (!noclip && collidesAt(world, player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) {
127	    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) {
128	      player.position.y += 0.2;
129	      if (!collidesAt(world, player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT)) break;
130	    }
131	  }
132	
133	  // While flying, crouch means "descend" — don't snap the player back from edges.
134	  if (!flying && crouching && (player.onGround || wasGrounded) && !hasSupportUnderPlayer(world, player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH + 0.12)) {
135	    player.position.x = prevX;
136	    player.position.z = prevZ;
137	    player.velocity.x = 0;
138	    player.velocity.z = 0;
139	  }
140	
141	  player.position.x = Math.min(world.sizeX - WORLD_BORDER_PADDING, Math.max(WORLD_BORDER_PADDING, player.position.x));
142	  player.position.z = Math.min(world.sizeZ - WORLD_BORDER_PADDING, Math.max(WORLD_BORDER_PADDING, player.position.z));
143	
144	  if (!wasGrounded && player.onGround && vyBeforeMove < -14) {
145	    // Any fall hard enough to trigger this deals at least half a heart.
146	    applyDamage(Math.max(1, Math.min(19, Math.floor((-vyBeforeMove - 13) * 0.5))));
147	  }
148	
149	  const inVoid = player.position.y < -4;
150	  if (inVoid) {
151	    timers.voidTimer += dt;
152	    if (timers.voidTimer >= 0.4) {
153	      applyDamage(1);
154	      timers.voidTimer = 0;
155	    }
156	  } else {
157	    timers.voidTimer = 0;
158	  }
159	
160	  const horizontalDistance = Math.hypot(player.position.x - startX, player.position.z - startZ);
161	  const moving = horizontalDistance > 1e-4;
162	  const didSprint = sprinting && moving;
163	  const didWalk = !didSprint && moving;
164	  const didLand = !wasGrounded && player.onGround && vyBeforeMove < 0;
165	  return { didSprint, didWalk, didJump, didLand, landImpact: didLand ? -vyBeforeMove : 0, horizontalDistance };
166	}
167
```

```
1	import { MAX_HEARTS, MAX_HUNGER, RESPAWN_SECONDS } from "@/lib/game/config";
2	import { takesDamage } from "@/lib/game/gameModes";
3	import { armorReduction, consumeEquippedArmorDurability } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
4	import type { GameState } from "../state";
5	
6	/**
7	 * Applies armor-mitigated damage (always at least 1) and wears equipped armor.
8	 * Returns true when the hit was lethal — the engine emits the death event.
9	 * Creative/Spectator are invulnerable, so every damage source no-ops here.
10	 */
11	export function applyDamageWithArmor(state: GameState, amount: number, rng?: () => number): boolean {
12	  if (state.isDead || !takesDamage(state.gameMode)) return false;
13	  const value = Math.max(0, Math.floor(amount));
14	  if (value <= 0) return false;
15	
16	  state.equippedArmor = consumeEquippedArmorDurability(state.equippedArmor, 1, rng) ?? state.equippedArmor;
17	  const reduction = armorReduction(state.equippedArmor);
18	  const mitigated = Math.max(1, Math.floor(value * (1 - reduction)));
19	
20	  state.hearts = Math.max(0, state.hearts - mitigated);
21	  if (state.hearts > 0) return false;
22	
23	  state.isDead = true;
24	  state.respawnTimer = RESPAWN_SECONDS;
25	  return true;
26	}
27	
28	/** Applies environmental damage exactly, bypassing armor and durability wear. */
29	export function applyUnmitigatedDamage(state: GameState, amount: number): boolean {
30	  if (state.isDead || !takesDamage(state.gameMode)) return false;
31	  const value = Math.max(0, Math.floor(amount));
32	  if (value <= 0) return false;
33	
34	  state.hearts = Math.max(0, state.hearts - value);
35	  if (state.hearts > 0) return false;
36	
37	  state.isDead = true;
38	  state.respawnTimer = RESPAWN_SECONDS;
39	  return true;
40	}
41	
42	/**
43	 * Applies armor-bypassing damage that can never be lethal: hearts never drop
44	 * below `floorHp`. Used by Poison, which should chip a player down to half a
45	 * heart yet never deliver the killing blow. Returns true when any damage landed.
46	 */
47	export function applyNonLethalDamage(state: GameState, amount: number, floorHp = 1): boolean {
48	  if (state.isDead || !takesDamage(state.gameMode)) return false;
49	  const value = Math.max(0, Math.floor(amount));
50	  if (value <= 0 || state.hearts <= floorHp) return false;
51	  state.hearts = Math.max(floorHp, state.hearts - value);
52	  return true;
53	}
54	
55	/**
56	 * Counts down the respawn timer while dead. Returns true when the player
57	 * comes back to life this tick — the engine then performs the respawn.
58	 */
59	export function tickRespawnTimer(state: GameState, dt: number): boolean {
60	  if (!state.isDead) return false;
61	  state.respawnTimer -= dt;
62	  if (state.respawnTimer > 0) return false;
63	
64	  state.hearts = MAX_HEARTS;
65	  state.hunger = MAX_HUNGER;
66	  state.isDead = false;
67	  state.respawnTimer = 0;
68	  state.timers.waterExposureTimer = 0;
69	  state.timers.waterDamageTimer = 0;
70	  return true;
71	}
72
```

```
757	  private removeMobAt = (index: number): void => {
758	    const state = this.state;
759	    const mob = state.mobs[index];
760	    state.mobs.splice(index, 1);
761	    // Babies drop nothing — only grown animals yield loot.
762	    const drops = mob.ageTimer <= 0 ? rollMobDrops(mob.kind, this.rng) : [];
763	    for (const drop of drops) {
764	      state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
765	    }
766	    // XP on kill, baby-gated like drops; the boss pays a jackpot.
767	    if (mob.ageTimer <= 0) awardXp(state, xpForMob(mob.kind), this.emit);
768	    this.emit({ type: "mobDied", kind: mob.kind, x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
769	    // Drops land straight in inventory (no ground item), so announce them.
770	    if (drops.length > 0) this.emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: drops });
771	    // Defeating the boss is the win condition — fire the one-shot victory.
772	    if (mob.kind === "boss") {
773	      state.victory = true;
774	      this.emit({ type: "bossDefeated", x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
775	    }
776	  };
```

```
1	import PixelImg from "@/components/game/PixelImg";
2	import { hudIconUrl } from "@/lib/ui/sprites";
3	import type { HudIconName } from "@/lib/ui/hudPixels";
4	import type { EffectId } from "@/lib/game/types";
5	
6	type ActiveEffect = { id: EffectId; seconds: number };
7	
8	type ActiveEffectsProps = {
9	  effects: ActiveEffect[];
10	};
11	
12	const ICON_SIZE = 22;
13	
14	const EFFECT_ICONS: Record<EffectId, HudIconName> = {
15	  speed: "effect_speed",
16	  strength: "effect_strength",
17	  regeneration: "effect_regeneration",
18	  fire_resistance: "effect_fire_resistance",
19	  water_breathing: "effect_water_breathing",
20	  poison: "effect_poison"
21	};
22	
23	const EFFECT_LABELS: Record<EffectId, string> = {
24	  speed: "Swiftness",
25	  strength: "Strength",
26	  regeneration: "Regeneration",
27	  fire_resistance: "Fire Resistance",
28	  water_breathing: "Water Breathing",
29	  poison: "Poison"
30	};
31	
32	/** Minecraft-style m:ss countdown (e.g. 2:05). */
33	function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
34	  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds));
35	  const minutes = Math.floor(total / 60);
36	  const rest = total % 60;
37	  return `${minutes}:${rest.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
38	}
39	
40	/**
41	 * The active status-effect readout: a stacked column of icon + countdown chips
42	 * in the top-left, opposite the minimap. Hidden entirely when nothing is active.
43	 */
44	export default function ActiveEffects({ effects }: ActiveEffectsProps) {
45	  if (effects.length === 0) return null;
46	  return (
47	    <div className="active-effects" role="list" aria-label="Active effects">
48	      {effects.map((effect) => (
49	        <div key={effect.id} className="active-effect" role="listitem" aria-label={`${EFFECT_LABELS[effect.id]}: ${formatDuration(effect.seconds)}`}>
50	          <PixelImg src={hudIconUrl(EFFECT_ICONS[effect.id])} alt="" size={ICON_SIZE} aria-hidden />
51	          <span className="active-effect-time">{formatDuration(effect.seconds)}</span>
52	        </div>
53	      ))}
54	    </div>
55	  );
56	}
57
```

```
291	    case "heart_hardcore_full":

297	    case "hunger_full":
298	      return paintGrid(DRUMSTICK_GRID, DRUMSTICK_PALETTE);
299	    case "hunger_container":
```

```
5	const ALL_ICONS: HudIconName[] = [
6	  "heart_full",
7	  "heart_half",
8	  "heart_container",
9	  "hunger_full",
10	  "hunger_half",
11	  "hunger_container",
12	  "armor_full",

15	  "bubble_full",
16	  "bubble_half",
17	  "bubble_container"
18	];

20	function opaquePixelCount(pixels: Uint8ClampedArray): number {
21	  let count = 0;
22	  for (let i = 3; i < pixels.length; i += 4) if (pixels[i] > 0) count += 1;
23	  return count;
24	}
25	
26	describe("renderHudIconPixels", () => {
27	  test("every icon renders non-empty 16x16 pixels", () => {
28	    for (const name of ALL_ICONS) {
29	      const pixels = renderHudIconPixels(name);
30	      expect(pixels.length).toBe(SPRITE_SIZE * SPRITE_SIZE * 4);
31	      expect(opaquePixelCount(pixels)).toBeGreaterThan(20);
32	    }
33	  });
34	
35	  test("full, half, and container variants differ", () => {
36	    expect(renderHudIconPixels("heart_full")).not.toEqual(renderHudIconPixels("heart_container"));
37	    expect(renderHudIconPixels("heart_half")).not.toEqual(renderHudIconPixels("heart_full"));
38	    expect(renderHudIconPixels("heart_half")).not.toEqual(renderHudIconPixels("heart_container"));
39	    expect(renderHudIconPixels("bubble_full")).not.toEqual(renderHudIconPixels("bubble_container"));
40	    expect(renderHudIconPixels("bubble_half")).not.toEqual(renderHudIconPixels("bubble_full"));
41	  });
42	
43	  test("the half heart keeps the full color only on the left side", () => {
44	    const half = renderHudIconPixels("heart_half");
45	    const full = renderHudIconPixels("heart_full");
46	    const container = renderHudIconPixels("heart_container");
47	    const mid = 6; // row 6 crosses the heart body
48	    const leftIdx = (mid * SPRITE_SIZE + 3) * 4;
49	    const rightIdx = (mid * SPRITE_SIZE + 12) * 4;
50	    expect(half[leftIdx]).toBe(full[leftIdx]);
51	    expect(half[rightIdx]).toBe(container[rightIdx]);
52	  });
53	});
54
```

```
5	import { miningSpeed } from "@/lib/game/engine/systems/mining";
6	import { applyEnchant, canEnchant, efficiencyMultiplier, enchantLevel, mendXp, sharpnessBonus, unbreakingSkips } from "@/lib/game/enchantments";
7	import type { EquippedArmor, InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";
8	
9	const withEnchant = (id: string, level: number, slot: InventorySlot): InventorySlot => ({ ...slot, enchantments: [{ id: id as never, level }] });
10	
11	describe("canEnchant / applyEnchant", () => {
12	  test("gates by item kind", () => {
13	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_sword", 1), "sharpness")).toBe(true);
14	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1), "sharpness")).toBe(false); // tool, not weapon
15	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1), "efficiency")).toBe(true);
16	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("helmet", 1), "protection")).toBe(true);
17	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("helmet", 1), "efficiency")).toBe(false);
18	    // Unbreaking and Mending apply to all three durable kinds.
19	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_sword", 1), "unbreaking")).toBe(true);
20	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1), "unbreaking")).toBe(true);
21	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("helmet", 1), "unbreaking")).toBe(true);
22	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_sword", 1), "mending")).toBe(true);
23	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1), "mending")).toBe(true);
24	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("helmet", 1), "mending")).toBe(true);
25	    // Non-durable / empty slots can't be enchanted.
26	    expect(canEnchant(createSlot("dirt", 1), "unbreaking")).toBe(false);
27	  });
28	
29	  test("applyEnchant adds then levels up, immutably, and stops at the cap", () => {
30	    const sword = createSlot("diamond_sword", 1);
31	    const once = applyEnchant(sword, "sharpness");
32	    expect(enchantLevel(once, "sharpness")).toBe(1);
33	    expect(sword.enchantments).toBeUndefined(); // original untouched
34	
35	    const twice = applyEnchant(once, "sharpness");
36	    expect(enchantLevel(twice, "sharpness")).toBe(2);
37	
38	    let maxed = sword;
39	    for (let i = 0; i < ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL + 2; i += 1) maxed = applyEnchant(maxed, "sharpness");
40	    expect(enchantLevel(maxed, "sharpness")).toBe(ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL);
41	    expect(canEnchant(maxed, "sharpness")).toBe(false);
42	  });
43	});
44	
45	describe("seam readers", () => {
46	  test("Sharpness adds flat melee damage per level", () => {
47	    expect(sharpnessBonus(createSlot("diamond_sword", 1))).toBe(0);
48	    expect(sharpnessBonus(withEnchant("sharpness", 2, createSlot("diamond_sword", 1)))).toBe(2 * SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL);
49	  });
50	
51	  test("Efficiency multiplies mining speed", () => {
52	    const plain = createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1);
53	    const enchanted = withEnchant("efficiency", 1, plain);
54	    expect(efficiencyMultiplier(plain)).toBe(1);
55	    expect(miningSpeed(enchanted)).toBeCloseTo(miningSpeed(plain) * (1 + EFFICIENCY_SPEED_PER_LEVEL), 5);
56	  });
57	
58	  test("Protection raises equipped defense and damage reduction", () => {
59	    const plain: EquippedArmor = { ...createEmptyArmorEquipment(), helmet: createSlot("helmet", 1) };
60	    const enchanted: EquippedArmor = { ...createEmptyArmorEquipment(), helmet: withEnchant("protection", 2, createSlot("helmet", 1)) };
61	    expect(equippedDefense(enchanted)).toBeGreaterThan(equippedDefense(plain));
62	    expect(armorReduction(enchanted)).toBeGreaterThan(armorReduction(plain));
63	  });
64	});
65	
66	describe("Unbreaking", () => {
67	  test("a roll under level × skip chance skips wear, otherwise it wears (and no rng always wears)", () => {
68	    const tool = withEnchant("unbreaking", 3, createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1)); // skip chance 0.6
69	    const slots = [tool];
70	    const max = tool.maxDurability!;
71	
72	    expect(unbreakingSkips(tool, () => 0)).toBe(true);
73	    expect(consumeToolDurability(slots, 0, 1, () => 0)).toBeNull(); // saved — no change
74	
75	    expect(unbreakingSkips(tool, () => 0.99)).toBe(false);
76	    expect(consumeToolDurability(slots, 0, 1, () => 0.99)?.[0].durability).toBe(max - 1);
77	
78	    // Back-compat: without an rng, Unbreaking never triggers.
79	    expect(consumeToolDurability(slots, 0, 1)?.[0].durability).toBe(max - 1);
80	  });
81	});
82	
83	describe("Mending (mendXp)", () => {
84	  const damaged = (id: string, missing: number): InventorySlot => {
85	    const slot = applyEnchant(createSlot(id, 1), "mending");
86	    slot.durability = slot.maxDurability! - missing;
87	    return slot;
88	  };
89	  const noArmor = createEmptyArmorEquipment();
90	
91	  test("repairs the held item first, capping at max durability and returning the XP left", () => {
92	    const missing = 6;
93	    const slots = [damaged("diamond_pickaxe", missing)];
94	    const xpNeeded = Math.ceil(missing / MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP); // 3
95	    const result = mendXp(slots, 0, noArmor, xpNeeded + 4);
96	    expect(result.slots[0].durability).toBe(slots[0].maxDurability!); // fully repaired
97	    expect(result.xpLeft).toBe(4);
98	    expect(result.slots).not.toBe(slots); // cloned on write
99	  });
100	
101	  test("never repairs above max and never goes negative on XP", () => {
102	    const slots = [damaged("diamond_pickaxe", 2)];
103	    const result = mendXp(slots, 0, noArmor, 100);
104	    expect(result.slots[0].durability).toBe(slots[0].maxDurability);
105	    expect(result.xpLeft).toBe(100 - Math.ceil(2 / MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP));
106	  });
107	
108	  test("falls through to worn Mending armor after the held item", () => {
109	    const helmet = damaged("helmet", 4);
110	    const slots = [createSlot("diamond_sword", 1)]; // held sword has no mending
111	    const equipped: EquippedArmor = { ...noArmor, helmet };
112	    const result = mendXp(slots, 0, equipped, 50);
113	    expect(result.equipped.helmet!.durability).toBe(helmet.maxDurability);
114	  });
115	
116	  test("no Mending gear → same array ref and full XP banked", () => {
117	    const slots = [createSlot("diamond_pickaxe", 1)];
118	    const result = mendXp(slots, 0, noArmor, 9);
119	    expect(result.slots).toBe(slots);
120	    expect(result.xpLeft).toBe(9);
121	  });
122	});
123
```

```
1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
2	import { ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
3	import { MOB_DROPS, rollMobDrops } from "@/lib/game/mobLoot";
4	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
5	
6	// Derived from the table so new mob kinds are covered automatically.
7	const ALL_KINDS = Object.keys(MOB_DROPS) as MobKind[];
8	
9	describe("mob drop tables", () => {
10	  test("every drop references an existing item", () => {
11	    for (const kind of ALL_KINDS) {
12	      for (const entry of MOB_DROPS[kind]) {
13	        expect(ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[entry.itemId]).toBeDefined();
14	        expect(entry.min).toBeLessThanOrEqual(entry.max);
15	        expect(entry.min).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
16	      }
17	    }
18	  });
19	
20	  test("rng at 0 yields the minimum count of each guaranteed entry", () => {
21	    // rng() === 0 takes any chance entry and floors the count to its minimum.
22	    const drops = rollMobDrops("sheep", () => 0);
23	    const byId = new Map(drops.map((d) => [d.itemId, d.count]));
24	    expect(byId.get("wool")).toBe(1); // min 1
25	    expect(byId.get("raw_mutton")).toBe(1); // min 1
26	  });
27	
28	  test("rng near 1 yields the maximum count", () => {
29	    const rng = () => 0.999;
30	    const drops = rollMobDrops("sheep", rng);
31	    const byId = new Map(drops.map((d) => [d.itemId, d.count]));
32	    expect(byId.get("wool")).toBe(2); // max 2
33	    expect(byId.get("raw_mutton")).toBe(1);
34	  });
35	
36	  test("a zero-minimum entry can drop nothing at the low roll", () => {
37	    // chicken feather is min 0; at rng 0 the count floors to 0 and is omitted.
38	    const drops = rollMobDrops("chicken", () => 0);
39	    expect(drops.some((d) => d.itemId === "feather")).toBe(false);
40	    expect(drops.some((d) => d.itemId === "raw_chicken")).toBe(true);
41	  });
42	
43	  test("every combat mob has at least one drop entry (villagers are NPCs and drop nothing)", () => {
44	    for (const kind of ALL_KINDS) {
45	      if (kind === "villager") {
46	        expect(MOB_DROPS[kind]).toHaveLength(0); // intentional: don't reward killing a trader
47	        continue;
48	      }
49	      expect(MOB_DROPS[kind].length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
50	    }
51	  });
52	});
```

```
1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
2	import * as THREE from "three";
3	import {
4	  EFFECT_REGEN_INTERVAL,
5	  EFFECT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER,
6	  EFFECT_STRENGTH_BONUS,
7	  MAX_HEARTS,
8	  MAX_OXYGEN,
9	  POISON_FLOOR_HP,
10	  POISON_INTERVAL
11	} from "@/lib/game/config";
12	import { BlockId } from "@/lib/world";
13	import { createTimers, type GameEvent, type GameState } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
14	import {
15	  addEffect,
16	  clearEffects,
17	  effectRemaining,
18	  hasEffect,
19	  speedMultiplier,
20	  strengthBonus,
21	  tickStatusEffects,
22	  type StatusEffectDeps
23	} from "@/lib/game/engine/systems/statusEffects";
24	import { applyNonLethalDamage } from "@/lib/game/engine/systems/playerLife";
25	import { tickLavaExposure, tickOxygen } from "@/lib/game/engine/systems/playerStats";
26	import type { EffectId } from "@/lib/game/types";
27	
28	function makeState(overrides: Partial<GameState> = {}): GameState {
29	  return {
30	    player: { position: new THREE.Vector3(0, 64, 0), velocity: new THREE.Vector3(), yaw: 0, pitch: 0, onGround: true },
31	    gameMode: "survival",
32	    hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
33	    hunger: 20,
34	    oxygen: MAX_OXYGEN,
35	    isDead: false,
36	    effects: new Map<EffectId, number>(),
37	    timers: createTimers(),
38	    ...overrides
39	  } as unknown as GameState;
40	}
41	
42	/** A world stub whose every cell is `block` — enough for the lava/oxygen gates. */
43	function uniformWorld(block: BlockId): GameState["world"] {
44	  return { get: () => block } as unknown as GameState["world"];
45	}
46	
47	function deps(overrides: Partial<StatusEffectDeps> = {}): StatusEffectDeps {
48	  return { applyPoisonDamage: () => {}, emit: () => {}, ...overrides };
49	}
50	
51	describe("addEffect / hasEffect / effectRemaining", () => {
52	  test("adds an effect and reports its remaining time", () => {
53	    const state = makeState();
54	    addEffect(state, "speed", 30);
55	    expect(hasEffect(state, "speed")).toBe(true);
56	    expect(effectRemaining(state, "speed")).toBe(30);
57	    expect(hasEffect(state, "strength")).toBe(false);
58	  });
59	
60	  test("refresh keeps the longer of the two durations", () => {
61	    const state = makeState();
62	    addEffect(state, "speed", 30);
63	    addEffect(state, "speed", 10); // shorter — ignored
64	    expect(effectRemaining(state, "speed")).toBe(30);
65	    addEffect(state, "speed", 45); // longer — wins
66	    expect(effectRemaining(state, "speed")).toBe(45);
67	  });
68	});
69	
70	describe("modifiers", () => {
71	  test("speedMultiplier and strengthBonus reflect active effects", () => {
72	    const state = makeState();
73	    expect(speedMultiplier(state)).toBe(1);
74	    expect(strengthBonus(state)).toBe(0);
75	    addEffect(state, "speed", 10);
76	    addEffect(state, "strength", 10);
77	    expect(speedMultiplier(state)).toBe(EFFECT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER);
78	    expect(strengthBonus(state)).toBe(EFFECT_STRENGTH_BONUS);
79	  });
80	});
81	
82	describe("tickStatusEffects", () => {
83	  test("counts effects down and emits effectExpired at zero", () => {
84	    const state = makeState();
85	    addEffect(state, "speed", 1.0);
86	    const events: GameEvent[] = [];
87	    const d = deps({ emit: (e) => events.push(e) });
88	
89	    tickStatusEffects(state, 0.4, d);
90	    expect(effectRemaining(state, "speed")).toBeCloseTo(0.6, 5);
91	    tickStatusEffects(state, 0.4, d);
92	    tickStatusEffects(state, 0.4, d); // 1.2s total → expired
93	    expect(hasEffect(state, "speed")).toBe(false);
94	    expect(events).toEqual([{ type: "effectExpired", effect: "speed" }]);
95	  });
96	
97	  test("regeneration heals on its own cadence even at zero hunger", () => {
98	    const state = makeState({ hearts: 10, hunger: 0 });
99	    addEffect(state, "regeneration", 100);
100	    tickStatusEffects(state, EFFECT_REGEN_INTERVAL, deps());
101	    expect(state.hearts).toBe(11); // healed despite hunger gating regen off
102	  });
103	
104	  test("regeneration never exceeds the heart cap", () => {
105	    const state = makeState({ hearts: MAX_HEARTS, hunger: 0 });
106	    addEffect(state, "regeneration", 100);
107	    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) tickStatusEffects(state, EFFECT_REGEN_INTERVAL, deps());
108	    expect(state.hearts).toBe(MAX_HEARTS);
109	  });
110	
111	  test("poison chips hearts down but floors at half a heart", () => {
112	    const state = makeState({ hearts: 3 });
113	    addEffect(state, "poison", 100);
114	    const d = deps({ applyPoisonDamage: (amount) => applyNonLethalDamage(state, amount, POISON_FLOOR_HP) });
115	    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i += 1) tickStatusEffects(state, POISON_INTERVAL, d);
116	    expect(state.hearts).toBe(POISON_FLOOR_HP); // chipped to the floor, never below
117	  });
118	});
119	
120	describe("clearEffects", () => {
121	  test("drops every effect and resets the accumulators", () => {
122	    const state = makeState();
123	    addEffect(state, "speed", 30);
124	    addEffect(state, "poison", 8);
125	    state.timers.effectRegenTimer = 0.7;
126	    state.timers.effectPoisonTimer = 0.9;
127	    clearEffects(state);
128	    expect(state.effects.size).toBe(0);
129	    expect(state.timers.effectRegenTimer).toBe(0);
130	    expect(state.timers.effectPoisonTimer).toBe(0);
131	  });
132	});
133	
134	describe("applyNonLethalDamage", () => {
135	  test("floors hearts and never returns a kill", () => {
136	    const state = makeState({ hearts: 2 });
137	    expect(applyNonLethalDamage(state, 5, 1)).toBe(true);
138	    expect(state.hearts).toBe(1);
139	    expect(applyNonLethalDamage(state, 5, 1)).toBe(false); // already at the floor
140	    expect(state.hearts).toBe(1);
141	    expect(state.isDead).toBe(false); // never set lethal in this helper
142	  });
143	});
144	
145	describe("environmental gates", () => {
146	  test("fire resistance negates lava burn", () => {
147	    const state = makeState({ world: uniformWorld(BlockId.Lava) });
148	    let damage = 0;
149	    tickLavaExposure(state, 0.5, (a) => (damage += a), true);
150	    expect(damage).toBe(0);
151	    tickLavaExposure(state, 0.5, (a) => (damage += a), false);
152	    expect(damage).toBeGreaterThan(0);
153	  });
154	
155	  test("water breathing keeps the lungs full and immune to drowning", () => {
156	    const state = makeState({ world: uniformWorld(BlockId.Water), oxygen: 0 });
157	    let damage = 0;
158	    tickOxygen(state, 1, (a) => (damage += a), true);
159	    expect(damage).toBe(0);

162	    state.oxygen = 0;
163	    tickOxygen(state, 1, (a) => (damage += a), false);
164	    expect(damage).toBeGreaterThan(0);
165	  });
166	});
```

```
10	function makeState(slots: InventorySlot[], selectedSlot = 0): GameState {
11	  return {
12	    player: { position: new THREE.Vector3(0, 64, 0), velocity: new THREE.Vector3(), yaw: 0, pitch: 0, onGround: true },
13	    inventory: slots,
14	    selectedSlot,
15	    timers: createTimers(),
16	    projectiles: [],
17	    nextProjectileId: 1
18	  } as unknown as GameState;
19	}
20	
21	function inventory(items: Array<[string, number]>): InventorySlot[] {
22	  const slots = Array.from({ length: 9 }, () => createEmptySlot());
23	  items.forEach(([id, count], i) => {
24	    slots[i] = createSlot(id, count);
25	  });
26	  return slots;
27	}

29	describe("isBow", () => {
30	  test("recognizes a held bow and rejects other items", () => {
31	    expect(isBow(createSlot("bow", 1))).toBe(true);
32	    expect(isBow(createSlot("diamond_sword", 1))).toBe(false);
33	    expect(isBow(createEmptySlot())).toBe(false);
34	    expect(isBow(undefined)).toBe(false);
35	  });
36	});

38	describe("tryFireBow", () => {
39	  test("fires an arrow, consumes ammo + durability, and arms the cooldown", () => {
40	    const state = makeState(
41	      inventory([
42	        ["bow", 1],
43	        ["arrow", 5]
44	      ])
45	    );
46	    const events: GameEvent[] = [];
47	    const fired = tryFireBow(state, (e) => events.push(e));
48	
49	    expect(fired).toBe(true);
50	    expect(state.projectiles).toHaveLength(1);
51	    expect(state.projectiles[0].fromPlayer).toBe(true);
52	    expect(countsById(state.inventory).get("arrow")).toBe(4);
53	    expect(state.inventory[0].durability).toBe(199); // 200 max − 1 per shot
54	    expect(state.timers.bowCooldownTimer).toBeCloseTo(BOW_COOLDOWN_SECONDS, 5);
55	    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === "bowFired")).toBe(true);
56	  });
57	
58	  test("refuses to fire while on cooldown", () => {
59	    const state = makeState(
60	      inventory([
61	        ["bow", 1],
62	        ["arrow", 5]
63	      ])
64	    );
65	    expect(tryFireBow(state, () => {})).toBe(true);
66	    const after = state.projectiles.length;
67	    expect(tryFireBow(state, () => {})).toBe(false);
68	    expect(state.projectiles).toHaveLength(after); // no second arrow
69	  });
70

73	    expect(tryFireBow(state, () => {})).toBe(false);
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "export type EnchantmentDef = {\n  id: EnchantmentId;\n  label: string;\n  /** Item kinds this enchantment can be applied to. */\n  kinds: ItemKind[];\n  maxLevel: number;\n};\n\nexport const ENCHANTMENT_DEFS: Record<EnchantmentId, EnchantmentDef> = {\n  sharpness: { id: \"sharpness\", label: \"Sharpness\", kinds: [\"weapon\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  protection: { id: \"protection\", label: \"Protection\", kinds: [\"armor\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  efficiency: { id: \"efficiency\", label: \"Efficiency\", kinds: [\"tool\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  unbreaking: { id: \"unbreaking\", label: \"Unbreaking\", kinds: [\"tool\", \"weapon\", \"armor\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  mending: { id: \"mending\", label: \"Mending\", kinds: [\"tool\", \"weapon\", \"armor\"], maxLevel: MENDING_MAX_LEVEL }\n};\n\n/** Stable display order for the enchanting panel. */\nexport const ENCHANTMENT_ORDER: readonly EnchantmentId[] = [\"sharpness\", \"protection\", \"efficiency\", \"unbreaking\", \"mending\"];",
  "new_string": "export type EnchantmentDef = {\n  id: EnchantmentId;\n  label: string;\n  /** Item kinds this enchantment can be applied to. */\n  kinds: ItemKind[];\n  /**\n   * Optional allow-list of specific item ids: when set, the slot's item must be\n   * one of these (on top of the `kinds` gate). Used to bind a weapon enchant to a\n   * single item — Power/Punch are bow-only, not for swords (which also are `weapon`).\n   */\n  itemIds?: string[];\n  maxLevel: number;\n};\n\nexport const ENCHANTMENT_DEFS: Record<EnchantmentId, EnchantmentDef> = {\n  sharpness: { id: \"sharpness\", label: \"Sharpness\", kinds: [\"weapon\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  protection: { id: \"protection\", label: \"Protection\", kinds: [\"armor\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  efficiency: { id: \"efficiency\", label: \"Efficiency\", kinds: [\"tool\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  unbreaking: { id: \"unbreaking\", label: \"Unbreaking\", kinds: [\"tool\", \"weapon\", \"armor\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  mending: { id: \"mending\", label: \"Mending\", kinds: [\"tool\", \"weapon\", \"armor\"], maxLevel: MENDING_MAX_LEVEL },\n  power: { id: \"power\", label: \"Power\", kinds: [\"weapon\"], itemIds: [\"bow\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL },\n  punch: { id: \"punch\", label: \"Punch\", kinds: [\"weapon\"], itemIds: [\"bow\"], maxLevel: ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL }\n};\n\n/** Stable display order for the enchanting panel. */\nexport const ENCHANTMENT_ORDER: readonly EnchantmentId[] = [\"sharpness\", \"power\", \"punch\", \"protection\", \"efficiency\", \"unbreaking\", \"mending\"];"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/enchantments.ts",
  "old_string": "/** Whether `id` can be applied to `slot` now — right item kind, owned, below its max level. */\nexport function canEnchant(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined, id: EnchantmentId): boolean {\n  if (!slot?.id || !slot.kind || slot.count <= 0) return false;\n  const def = ENCHANTMENT_DEFS[id];\n  return def.kinds.includes(slot.kind) && enchantLevel(slot, id) < def.maxLevel;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Whether `id` can be applied to `slot` now — right item kind (and specific item, if gated), owned, below its max level. */\nexport function canEnchant(slot: InventorySlot | null | undefined, id: EnchantmentId): boolean {\n  if (!slot?.id || !slot.kind || slot.count <= 0) return false;\n  const def = ENCHANTMENT_DEFS[id];\n  if (!def.kinds.includes(slot.kind)) return false;\n  if (def.itemIds && !def.itemIds.includes(slot.id)) return false; // item-specific enchant (e.g. bow-only Power/Punch)\n  return enchantLevel(slot, id) < def.maxLevel;\n}"
}
```

```
=== reference.md enchant table ===
82:| 1 Enchanting Table | 2 Diamond Ore + 4 Cobble |
142:| Neck Protection | 2 Gold Ore + 1 Sapphire Ore |
205:### Enchantments
208:(**3** for most, **1** for Mending), applied at the enchanting table to the
209:selected tool/weapon/armor. Enchantments are per-item and survive a save.
211:| Enchantment | Applies to          | Per level                                                     |
213:| Sharpness   | weapons             | +2 melee damage                                               |
214:| Protection  | armor               | +2 defense (more damage reduction)                            |
215:| Efficiency  | tools               | ×(1 + 0.3 × level) mining speed                               |
216:| Unbreaking  | tools/weapons/armor | 20% chance per level to skip wear                             |
217:| Mending     | tools/weapons/armor | gained XP repairs the gear (2 durability per XP); max level 1 |
241:| Enchanting Table  | 6        | any             | **Interactive** — opens the enchanting panel (spend XP levels). Crafted from 2 diamond ore + 4 cobble                             |
383:| Neck Protection | neck_protection | 2       | 230        |
=== tuning.md enchant ===
138:`XP_PER_LEVEL`, `FISHING_XP`, `ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL`, `ENCHANT_COST_LEVELS`,
139:`SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL`, `PROTECTION_DEFENSE_PER_LEVEL`,
145:`mobLoot.ts`); `FISHING_XP` (2) is the per-catch reward. Enchanting costs
146:`ENCHANT_COST_LEVELS` (3) levels per application, up to `ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL` (3).
148:Sharpness `+SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL` (2) melee damage (`combat.ts` dispatch),
151:speed (`miningSpeed`), Unbreaking a `UNBREAKING_SKIP_PER_LEVEL` (0.2)
153:and Mending (`MENDING_MAX_LEVEL` 1, binary) diverting gained XP to repair
=== manual.md enchant ===
350:- **Sharpness** (weapons) — hit harder in melee
352:- **Efficiency** (tools) — mine faster
353:- **Unbreaking** (any gear) — gear lasts longer (it sometimes ignores wear)
354:- **Mending** (any gear) — XP you pick up repairs the held or worn item instead of
358:Enchantments are part of the item, so they're kept in chests and across reloads.
379:enchant gear with **Mending** instead.
=== adding-content.md enchant section ===
81:- **A station with its own panel (not the recipe book)** — the **enchanting table**, the **anvil**, and the **grindstone** are the reference. Their interaction sets `state.craftingStation` to a new id, but that id goes on the **engine/UI** station union (`craftingStation` on `GameState` and `GameSnapshot`, and the `openedStation` event in `state.ts`, plus the `InventoryPanel` `craftingStation` prop) and **not** on `Recipe.station` — so `STATION_LABELS` (keyed off `Recipe.station`) needs no entry. The panel is a dedicated `*Column` component branched in before the recipe book in `InventoryPanel`, acting on the **selected hotbar slot** (`inventory[selectedHotbarSlot]`); its actions are new `Command`s in `commands.ts` handled in `GameEngine.dispatch` (gated on `state.craftingStation === <id>`), each emitting a one-shot event for the audio director. The block itself is **craft-only** (never generated), so adding the `BlockId` at the end of the enum needs **no `WORLDGEN_VERSION` bump** — just `BLOCK_COLORS` (atlas sizing), an atlas paint branch, the exhaustive `GROUP_BY_BLOCK`, and `BREAK_HARDNESS`/item-def/`BLOCK_TO_SLOT`/recipe.
125:## An enchantment (per-item gear modifier)
127:- Enchantments are **per-item-instance data, like durability** — they live on the `InventorySlot`'s `enchantments` field (`{ id, level }[]`), driven by `lib/game/enchantments.ts`. Add an id to the `EnchantmentId` union (`types.ts`), list it in `ENCHANTMENT_DEFS` (applicable item kinds + max level) and `ENCHANTMENT_ORDER`, and put magnitudes in `config.ts`.
129:- **Non-stackable for free**: enchantments only sit on durable gear, which is already stack-size 1 (`maxStackSizeForItem`), and `{ ...slot }` clones carry the field through every inventory move.
130:- **Apply it** with the `enchant` command (gated on an open enchanting table): `canEnchant` (kind + level cap) → `spendXpLevels` → immutable `applyEnchant`. The `EnchantingColumn` panel renders the options for the selected hotbar item.
131:- **Persist it**: enchantments are an **additive save field** on `SavedSlot` — see [save-format.md](save-format.md) (v7); `restoreSlot` validates them on load (known ids, clamped levels, durable gear only).
=== save-format.md mending/enchant ===
14:- inventory slots with durability, **enchantments, and a custom name** (36 slots; the first 9 are the hotbar). Each `SavedSlot` is `{ id, count, durability?, enchantments?, customName? }`; `enchantments` (`{ id, level }[]`) and `customName` (the anvil rename) ride durable gear only and are validated on load (`restoreSlot` — known enchant ids via `Object.hasOwn`, levels clamped to `1..ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL`; `customName` trimmed and capped to `CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN`, blanks dropped). The same path serializes chest contents
15:- `xp` (optional) — banked experience points (`XP_PER_LEVEL` points = one level). Earned from kills/ore/fishing, spent at the enchanting table. Restored by `restoreXp` (finite, ≥ 0). Unlike effects, XP is a currency and is **kept across death**
16:- equipped armor — the worn pieces themselves (one per slot, dedicated storage), persisted as per-slot `SavedSlot` via `serializeEquippedArmor`/`restoreEquippedArmor` (so worn durability/enchantments/customName survive). A worn piece lives **only** here, not in the 36 inventory slots
39:- **v12** (dedicated armor storage) — fixes a bug where equipped armor stayed in the inventory and occupied a hotbar/storage slot. `equippedArmor` is no longer a by-id reference into `inventorySlots` but the worn pieces themselves, persisted per slot as `SavedSlot` (via `serializeEquippedArmor`/`restoreEquippedArmor`, reusing the shared `restoreSlot` validation). `migrateSaveV11toV12` moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record (preserving durability/enchantments/customName; equips whose item isn't found or isn't valid armor are dropped; a legacy `inventoryCounts`-only save just drops the equip map and keeps the items). No worldgen change — **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8** and `SAVE_KEY` is unchanged.
40:- **v11** (anvil rename & Mending) — adds an optional `customName` on each `SavedSlot` (the anvil rename). `migrateSaveV10toV11` is a pure version bump (absent ⇒ no custom name). `customName` rides the existing `inventorySlotsSnapshot`/`restoreSlot` path (shared by inventory and chests), is restored on durable gear only, and is trimmed + capped to `CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN` with blanks dropped. The **Mending** enchantment adds no new field — it rides the existing per-slot `enchantments` (and its `mending` id is added to the save validator's known-id set). The **Anvil** and **Grindstone** items save by string id and their `BlockId`s are **appended** so saved block ids never shift. No worldgen change — both are craft-only — so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
44:- **v7** (XP & enchanting) — adds optional top-level `xp` and an optional `enchantments` array on each `SavedSlot`. `migrateSaveV6toV7` is a pure version bump (absent → xp 0, no enchantments). XP persists and is **kept across death** (it's a currency, not a status effect); enchantments ride the existing `inventorySlotsSnapshot`/`restoreSlot` path (shared by inventory and chests) and are validated on load. The **Enchanting Table** item saves by string id and the `EnchantingTable` `BlockId` is **appended** (id 52) so saved block ids never shift. No worldgen change — **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
53:- **v5** — adds optional `lootedChests` (opened/broken dungeon loot chests). `migrateSaveV4toV5` is a pure version bump (absent → no dungeon chests yet looted). Unlike v3/v4, **`SAVE_KEY` is bumped to `minecraft_save_v6`** here — not because of the save _shape_ (the field is additive) but because the dungeon worldgen changed the deterministic terrain baseline, so old block-diffs would index against the wrong world. Pre-dungeon saves are therefore discarded in practice; the migration keeps the version chain complete regardless. `readSave` chains v1 → v2 → v3 → v4 → v5 → v6 → v7 → v8 → v9 → v10 → v11 → v12.
=== CHANGELOG Unreleased ===
```

```
160	| Cooked Fish     | Raw Fish + Fuel     | Furnace |
161	
162	### Brewing & potions
163	
164	Each potion is one **glass bottle** plus one reagent, brewed at an open **brewing
165	stand**. Drink a potion with `F` (the eat key) to gain its [status effect](#status-effects);
166	drinking consumes the whole bottle. See the [manual](manual.md#brewing--potions) for the how-to.
167	
168	| Result                    | Ingredients              | Station |
169	| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------- |
170	| Potion of Swiftness       | Glass Bottle + Feather   | Brewing |
171	| Potion of Strength        | Glass Bottle + Gunpowder | Brewing |
172	| Potion of Regeneration    | Glass Bottle + Wheat     | Brewing |
173	| Potion of Fire Resistance | Glass Bottle + Coal      | Brewing |
174	| Potion of Water Breathing | Glass Bottle + Raw Fish  | Brewing |
175	
176	## Status effects
177	
178	Timed buffs on the player (`lib/game/engine/systems/statusEffects.ts`); their
179	icon + countdown show top-left while active. Re-applying refreshes to the longer
180	remaining time; **all clear on death** but otherwise persist across a reload.
181	
182	| Effect          | Duration | Source              | What it does                                           |
183	| --------------- | -------- | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
184	| Swiftness       | 3:00     | Potion (feather)    | Move ×1.2 faster                                       |
185	| Strength        | 3:00     | Potion (gunpowder)  | +3 melee damage per hit                                |
186	| Regeneration    | 0:45     | Potion (wheat)      | Heal 1 HP / 1.5 s, even at low hunger                  |
187	| Fire Resistance | 3:00     | Potion (coal)       | Lava can't burn you                                    |
188	| Water Breathing | 3:00     | Potion (raw fish)   | Lungs stay full; no drowning                           |
189	| Poison          | 0:08     | Eating rotten flesh | 1 HP / 1.25 s, but **never kills** (floors at ½ heart) |
190	
191	## XP & enchanting
192	
193	XP banks as points (`XP_PER_LEVEL` = 10 points per level) and is spent at an
194	**enchanting table** to enchant the selected gear. XP is **kept across death**.
195	Source: `lib/game/engine/systems/xp.ts`, `lib/game/mobXp.ts`, `lib/game/enchantments.ts`.
196	
197	### XP sources
198	
199	| Source        | XP                                                       |
200	| ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
201	| Mob kill      | passives 1–2, hostiles 5, **boss 200**; babies give none |
202	| Mining ore    | coal 1, sliver 2, ruby/gold 4, sapphire 6, diamond 8     |
203	| Fishing catch | 2                                                        |
204	
205	### Enchantments
206	
207	Each costs **3 XP levels** per application, up to that enchantment's max level
208	(**3** for most, **1** for Mending), applied at the enchanting table to the
209	selected tool/weapon/armor. Enchantments are per-item and survive a save.
210	
211	| Enchantment | Applies to          | Per level                                                     |
212	| ----------- | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
213	| Sharpness   | weapons             | +2 melee damage                                               |
214	| Protection  | armor               | +2 defense (more damage reduction)                            |
```

```
135	
136	## XP & enchanting
137	
138	`XP_PER_LEVEL`, `FISHING_XP`, `ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL`, `ENCHANT_COST_LEVELS`,
139	`SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL`, `PROTECTION_DEFENSE_PER_LEVEL`,
140	`EFFICIENCY_SPEED_PER_LEVEL`, `UNBREAKING_SKIP_PER_LEVEL`, `MENDING_MAX_LEVEL`,
141	`MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP`.
142	
143	XP banks as points; `XP_PER_LEVEL` (10) points make one level. The per-mob and
144	per-ore XP tables live in `mobXp.ts` / `systems/xp.ts` (not here, like
145	`mobLoot.ts`); `FISHING_XP` (2) is the per-catch reward. Enchanting costs
146	`ENCHANT_COST_LEVELS` (3) levels per application, up to `ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL` (3).
147	Each enchant is a flat per-level modifier read at one seam:
148	Sharpness `+SHARPNESS_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL` (2) melee damage (`combat.ts` dispatch),
149	Protection `+PROTECTION_DEFENSE_PER_LEVEL` (2) defense (`equippedDefense` →
150	`armorReduction`), Efficiency `×(1 + EFFICIENCY_SPEED_PER_LEVEL × level)` mining
151	speed (`miningSpeed`), Unbreaking a `UNBREAKING_SKIP_PER_LEVEL` (0.2)
152	skip-chance per level (`consumeToolDurability`/`consumeEquippedArmorDurability`),
153	and Mending (`MENDING_MAX_LEVEL` 1, binary) diverting gained XP to repair
154	`MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP` (2) durability per point on the held/worn item (`mendXp`,
155	read once in `awardXp`). Lower the costs or raise the magnitudes for faster
156	progression; the XP-source tables are the earning dial.
157	
158	## Anvil & grindstone
```

```
344	Spend it at an **enchanting table** (craft one from **2 diamond ore + 4 cobble**).
345	Place it, right-click to open the enchanting panel, then **select** the tool,
346	weapon, or armor you want to improve in your hotbar — the panel shows the
347	enchantments it can take. Each costs **3 levels** and can be applied up to **three
348	times**:
349	
350	- **Sharpness** (weapons) — hit harder in melee
351	- **Protection** (armor) — take less damage
352	- **Efficiency** (tools) — mine faster
353	- **Unbreaking** (any gear) — gear lasts longer (it sometimes ignores wear)
354	- **Mending** (any gear) — XP you pick up repairs the held or worn item instead of
355	  banking, as long as it's damaged
356	
357	Buttons grey out when you can't afford the cost or the enchant is maxed.
358	Enchantments are part of the item, so they're kept in chests and across reloads.
359	Full numbers are in the [reference](reference.md#xp--enchanting).
360	
361	## The anvil
362	
363	Craft an **anvil** (**3 gold ore + 4 cobble**), place it, and right-click to open
364	the anvil panel. Like the enchanting table it works on the **selected** hotbar
365	item — a tool, weapon, or armor piece — and spends XP levels:
```
